Conclusion: 通过引入器官条件化的模式-标记对齐，OCP-CT有效解决了全局对比的异质性模糊问题和器官级对齐的粗糙性问题，显著提升了CT视觉-语言预训练的零样本诊断性能。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) vision-language pretraining from paired volumes and radiology reports is a scalable yet challenging task. Existing methods commonly adopt global scan-report contrast, which is scalable but obscures heterogeneous organ evidence. Meanwhile, direct organ-level alignment remains coarse, since the same anatomy can exhibit multiple distinct radiological appearances. Therefore, pretraining requires a finer alignment unit: the organ-conditioned radiological pattern. In this work, we propose OCP-CT, an organ-conditioned pattern-token alignment framework for CT vision-language pretraining. Specifically, OCP-CT preserves a stable global CT-report contrastive branch and introduces an organ pattern interface: sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routes image and text tokens according to latent radiological patterns, learnable slots query the routed tokens into continuous pattern tokens, and paired token contrast aligns image-text pattern tokens with structured soft targets built from report-derived clinical similarity. On the publicly available CT-RATE and RAD-ChestCT benchmarks, OCP-CT achieves average AUROCs of 84.5% and 69.9% for zero-shot abnormality diagnosis, respectively. Compared with the strongest prior reported results, these results yield absolute AUROC gains of 6.7 and 0.8 percentage points.

</details>


### [106] [The 2nd International StepUP Competition for Biometric Footstep Recognition: From Steps to Strides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13905)
*Robyn Larracy,Anant Gupta,Gourav Gupta,Ethan Eddy,Maxime Devanne,Cyril Meyer,Jin-Chern Chiou,Yueh-Shan Lee,Zong-Han Lu,Aaron Tabor,Erik Scheme*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 第二届国际StepUP步态识别竞赛（基于压力脚印的生物特征识别）通过大规模StepUP-P150数据集（20万+高分辨率动态脚印，150人）和未公开测试集，评估了三个关键挑战：未见用户的小样本泛化、鞋类和速度变化导致的域偏移鲁棒性、左右脚印融合。最优方案（ArogyaPandit研究团队）用时序CNN+集成评分策略达到8.00%等错误率，表明时序模式利用和推理时归一化/校准策略有效，但未见个人鞋类识别仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动基于压力脚印的生物特征识别研究，通过标准化且具有挑战性的评估框架，解决实际应用中的泛化性、域偏移鲁棒性和双足融合问题。

Method: 使用StepUP-P150数据集（200,000+高分辨率动态脚印，150人）和未公开测试集，设置三个挑战任务：未见用户小样本泛化、鞋类和速度域偏移、左右脚印融合。采用步幅级验证（而非孤立脚印），引入更极端的跨域条件。竞赛面向学术界和工业界开放注册。

Result: 26支队伍注册参赛，最优方案（ArogyaPandit研究团队）采用时空CNN+集成评分策略，达到8.00%等错误率。

Conclusion: 时序CNN和推理时归一化/校准策略能有效提升脚印识别性能；但在未见个人鞋类且存在相似干扰项的情况下，用户识别仍面临显著挑战。

Abstract: The International StepUP Competition Series was launched to advance research in pressure-based footstep biometrics through a standardized and challenging evaluation framework. Using the large-scale StepUP-P150 dataset (with more than 200,000 high-resolution dynamic footsteps from 150 individuals) and a previously unreleased test set, the 2nd edition of the competition addressed three key challenges: (1) generalization to unseen users with limited enrollment data, (2) robustness to domain shift caused by variations in footwear and walking speed and (3) effective fusion of paired left-right footsteps. While the first two challenges built on the inaugural competition, this edition introduced more extreme cross-domain conditions and moved beyond isolated footsteps to stride-level verification, enabling new opportunities for representation learning and inter-step information fusion. The competition attracted 26 registrants from academia and industry, with a best equal error rate of 8.00% achieved by the ArogyaPandit Research Team using a spatiotemporal CNN combined with an ensemble-based scoring strategy. The top solutions showcase the value of harnessing temporal patterns and of incorporating inference-time normalization and calibration strategies to improve scoring. However, the results also reveal that recognizing users in unseen personal footwear remains a challenge, especially in the presence of distractors with similar characteristics.

</details>


### [107] [Thresholded Cross-Attention for Reliable Intensity-Chromaticity Fusion in Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13925)
*Yanyi Wu,Xu Zhang,Junkai Chen,Laibin Chang,Jiaqi Ma,Shi Chen,Linwei Zhu,Jianglei Di,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TCA-Net提出基于阈值化交叉注意力机制（Thresholded Cross-Attention, TCA）的低光照图像增强方法，通过自适应保留高置信度跨流交互、相位引导傅里叶交互模块和分离式双流引导模块，在HVI空间实现亮度-色度可靠融合，在多个基准数据集上取得了竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于HVI空间的方法通过分离亮度和色度来缓解颜色纠缠，但两流融合的可靠性被忽视。固定配额Top-K稀疏注意力与逐层置信度分布不匹配，导致丢弃重要依赖或保留噪声交互。

Method: (1) 核心：阈值化交叉注意力（TCA），用固定置信度阈值替代固定Top-K配额，实现输入和层自适应的交互保留；(2) 相位引导傅里叶交互模块（PFIM），为亮度流提供结构感知的亮度初始化；(3) 分离式双流引导模块（DDSGM），构建残差亮度特征抑制重建中的色度泄漏；(4) 尺度一致性正则化（SACR），增强训练中结构鲁棒性。

Result: 在LOL-v1、LOL-v2、Sony-Total-Dark和LSRW-Huawei数据集上，TCA-Net实现了具有竞争力的恢复精度、更好的颜色保真度和紧凑的参数规模。

Conclusion: 通过自适应阈值化的跨流注意力机制，有效解决了亮度-色度融合中置信度分布不均的问题，为低光照图像增强提供了一种高效且颜色保真的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-Light Image Enhancement (LLIE) requires a careful balance among noise suppression, color fidelity, and efficiency. Recent HVI-based methods alleviate color entanglement by decoupling intensity and chromaticity, yet how reliably the two streams are fused again is an overlooked factor that largely determines the final quality. We observe that the confidence of cross-stream attention is strongly layer-dependent, so the fixed-quota selection of Top-K sparse attention is mismatched to it, discarding informative dependencies in some layers while retaining noisy ones in others. Motivated by this observation, we propose TCA-Net, a network built around Thresholded Cross-Attention that targets reliable intensity-chromaticity fusion in the HVI space rather than introducing yet another color representation. At its core, TCA replaces the rigid Top-K quota with a fixed confidence threshold whose retained cardinality is input- and layer-adaptive, retaining only high-confidence cross-stream interactions while suppressing unreliable ones. Around this core, two complementary designs clean up the fusion before and after it: a Phase-guided Fourier Interaction Module provides a structure-aware brightness initialization for the intensity stream prior to fusion, and a Decoupled Dual-Stream Guidance Module constructs residual intensity features to suppress chromaticity leakage during reconstruction. A Scale-Aware Consistency Regularization further improves structural robustness under scale perturbations during training. Extensive experiments on LOL-v1, LOL-v2, Sony-Total-Dark, and LSRW-Huawei demonstrate that TCA-Net delivers competitive restoration accuracy, improved color fidelity, and a compact parameter size.

</details>


### [108] [Cyclone: Diffusion Model for Cycle-Consistent Weather Editing from Unpaired Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13927)
*Thang-Anh-Quan Nguyen,Moussab Bennehar,Luis Guillermo Roldao Jimenez,Nathan Piasco,Dzmitry Tsishkou,Laurent Caraffa,Jean-Philippe Tarel,Roland Brémond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Cyclone是一个基于潜扩散的统一天气编辑框架，利用循环一致性约束和图像-文本模型知识，无需配对数据即可生成多种真实天气效果，提升自动驾驶感知系统的鲁棒性，并支持蒸馏为视频扩散模型以保持时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统在多样化天气条件下的可靠感知仍是重大挑战。现有方法依赖合成数据增强或基于物理的任务特定模型，需要配对训练数据，且难以生成逼真的天气效果或在域外场景中泛化。

Method: 提出Cyclone框架，基于潜扩散模型，引入循环一致性约束，并利用图像-文本模型的知识，实现无需配对数据的多场景多天气条件编辑。

Result: 相比现有基线，Cyclone生成更真实、保结构的输出，并在多个下游驾驶感知任务中取得一致改进。此外，Cyclone可蒸馏为视频扩散模型，实现时序一致的天气编辑。

Conclusion: Cyclone作为一个统一框架，有效解决了天气编辑中配对数据依赖和泛化性不足的问题，显著提升了自动驾驶感知在多样化天气下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reliable perception under diverse weather conditions remains a major challenge for autonomous driving systems. A common strategy to improve robustness is either to synthesize adverse weather conditions for training perception models or to apply weather-removal techniques to recover clean inputs. However, existing approaches typically rely on synthetic data augmentation or physics-based, task-specific models that require paired training data and often struggle to generate realistic weather effects or generalize robustly to out-of-domain scenarios. Toward this problem, we present Cyclone, a unified framework for weather editing based on latent diffusion, equipped with cycle-consistent constraints and knowledge from image-text models. Cyclone enables the generation of multiple weather conditions across diverse scenes while eliminating the need for paired data. Experimental results show that our approach produces more realistic, structure-preserving outputs than existing baselines and leads to consistent improvements across several downstream driving perception tasks. Furthermore, we demonstrate that Cyclone can be distilled to a video diffusion model for temporally consistent weather editing.

</details>


### [109] [SIVA-RL: Sensitivity-Invariance Visual Alignment for Multimodal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13931)
*Cheng Tang,Junzhi Ning,Min Cen,Wei Li,Xinyi Zeng,Pinxian Zeng,Rongbin Li,Qiming Zhu,Yuqiang Li,Junjun He,Yirong Chen,Ming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SIVA-RL框架，通过样本级、结果条件的监督替代操作符条件的正则化，利用PatchSwap构建局部干预，使用冻结审计策略根据奖励下降程度进行软路由加权，在9个多模态推理基准上显著提升3B和7B模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉干预方法按干预类型分配监督，但相同操作在不同样本上产生异质结果，导致奖励信号不能保证模型真正基于视觉证据推理。

Method: 1) 通过token对齐、距离约束的PatchSwap构建局部干预；2) 冻结审计策略对清洁-干预对评分，根据奖励下降幅度进行软路由加权；3) 大下降对驱动敏感性对齐，低下降对驱动不变性对齐，模糊对降权；4) 兼容GRPO和DAPO主干。

Result: 在9个多模态推理基准上，3B和7B模型在所有设置中均优于匹配的RL基线；视觉依赖推理提升8.79个百分点，整体相对提升最高达14.9%。

Conclusion: SIVA-RL通过解耦干预构建与监督分配，实现了更有效的视觉接地，且主干无关。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) drives multimodal reasoning, but answer-level correctness does not guarantee that a vision-language model grounds its predictions in visual evidence. Existing visual-intervention methods contrast policy behavior on original and modified images, yet assign supervision by the type of intervention rather than its observed effect. This assumption fails: identical operators produce heterogeneous outcomes across samples. We propose SIVA-RL, a Sensitivity-Invariance Visual Alignment framework that replaces operator-conditioned regularization with sample-wise, outcome-conditioned supervision. SIVA-RL constructs localized interventions through token-aligned, distance-constrained within-image PatchSwap. A frozen audit policy then scores each clean-intervention pair, and the observed reward drop becomes soft routing weights. Large-drop pairs drive sensitivity alignment, low-drop pairs drive clean-anchored invariance alignment, and ambiguous pairs are down-weighted. This design decouples intervention construction from supervision assignment and is compatible with both GRPO and DAPO backbones. Across nine multimodal reasoning benchmarks spanning mathematical, logical, and vision-dependent tasks, SIVA-RL improves 3B and 7B models over matched RL baselines in every setting. It yields an 8.79 percentage-point gain on vision-dependent reasoning and up to 14.9% relative overall improvement across all four GRPO- and DAPO-based configurations.

</details>


### [110] [A novel unsupervised machine learning strategy to handle multimodal cardiac PET/MRI data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13936)
*Brunnhilde Ponsi,Thomas Carlier,Lara Marteau,Aurélien Monnet,Thomas Eugène,Jean-Michel Serfaty,Nicolas Piriou,Hatem Necib*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于PET/MRI多参数定量分析的聚类方法，通过两步聚类（先对每个患者的多模态影像进行超体素聚类，再对患者间超体素组进行谱聚类）生成异常评分，并自动生成文字和牛眼图报告，用于致心律失常性左心室心肌病的诊断。在99名患者和167个数值体模上验证，平衡精度分别达0.76±0.04和≥0.8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致心律失常性左心室心肌病缺乏金标准诊断标准，诊断困难；同时PET/MRI多模态影像数据缺乏系统性处理方法，难以有效表征心肌异质性和疾病表型。

Method: 对99名基因确诊患者的T1图、T2图、LGE和18F-FDG-PET图像进行独立z-score标准化并叠加，先对每个患者进行超体素聚类，再通过谱聚类获得32个患者间超体素组，为每个聚类-模态组合分配异常评分，自动生成文本和牛眼图报告，并与心脏影像医师评估进行平衡精度比较。

Result: 在患者数据集上重复嵌套交叉验证的平衡精度为0.76±0.04，在167个数值体模上平衡精度≥0.8。识别出的异常聚类与影像医师的视觉观察高度一致，有助于区分不同程度的纤维化或炎症区域。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了对多模态PET/MRI数据的系统性处理，能够有效表征致心律失常性左心室心肌病患者的心肌异质性，辅助临床诊断。

Abstract: Arrhythmogenic left ventricular cardiomyopathy is a genetic myocardial disease difficult to diagnose due to the lack of gold standard criteria. Simultaneous PET/MR imaging, combined with multiparametric quantitative analysis, could facilitate the identification of different profiles related to the phenotype and progression of cardiomyopathy. This preliminary study focuses on a methodological strategy for dealing with PET/MRI data, including inter-patient data linkage and regional analysis. Two-step clustering was applied to T1 and T2 maps, LGE, and 18F-FDG-PET images of 99 patients genetically diagnosed with arrhythmogenic left ventricular cardiomyopathy. Each patient's images were independently z-scored and summed into a single volume, which was clustered into supervoxels. Thirty-two inter-patient groups of supervoxels were obtained by spectral clustering. An "abnormality" score was assigned to each cluster and modality, and used to visualise abnormal regions likely associated with disease. They enabled the generation of automated textual and bullseye health reports for each patient, which were compared with cardiac imager assessments using balanced accuracy in repeated nested cross-validation. This approach was further validated on a larger cohort of 167 numerical phantoms. The reports generated by clustering accurately identified most of the cardiac physicians' observations (BA = 0.76 $\pm$ 0.04 in repeated nested cross-validation on patients, and BA $\ge$ 0.8 on phantoms). Furthermore, the identified abnormal clusters closely matched their visual observations, facilitating the identification of varying degrees of fibrosis or inflammation on the images. This approach enables a more systematic handling of multimodal PET/MRI data to characterise myocardial heterogeneity in arrhythmogenic left ventricular cardiomyopathy patients.

</details>


### [111] [Peak-End-Net: A Peak-End Rule Inspired Framework for Generalizable Video Aesthetic Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13941)
*Geng Li,Haiwen Li,Rui Chen,Jing Tang,Lei Sun,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Peak-End-Net，一种受心理学峰值-终末规则启发的轻量级视频美学评估框架。通过迁移图像美学评估知识生成帧级美学先验，结合美学节奏编码器和动态门控融合机制，在VADB和DIVIDE-3K基准上实现最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频美学评估（VAA）研究相对不足，主要面临两大挑战：缺乏大规模基准数据集，以及美学判断固有的主观性。本文从心理学视角重新审视VAA，旨在利用峰值-终末规则（人们主要根据最显著时刻和结尾来评判时间体验）构建更符合人类审美认知的评估模型。

Method: 1) 迁移IAA知识：使用预训练图像美学评估头生成帧级美学先验，作为识别美学显著时刻的代理信号；2) 峰值-终末规则聚合：基于帧级先验进行时间聚合；3) 美学节奏编码器：建模视频随时间演化的美学进度，超越孤立时刻；4) 动态门控融合机制：提升模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性。整体基于冻结的ViT，仅少量可训练参数。

Result: 在VADB（域内评估）和DIVIDE-3K（跨域测试）两个基准上均达到最先进性能，验证了心理学建模在VAA中的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于峰值-终末规则的心理建模能有效提升视频美学评估性能，且模型轻量、可解释性强，为VAA提供了新的方法论方向。

Abstract: Video aesthetic assessment (VAA) aims to predict how aesthetically pleasing a video is, yet remains far less explored than other visual assessment tasks. Its progress is hindered not only by the scarcity of large-scale benchmarks, but also by the intrinsic subjectivity of aesthetic judgment, which is shaped by human perception. In this paper, we revisit VAA from a psychological perspective and propose \textit{Peak-End-Net}, a lightweight and interpretable framework inspired by the \textit{peak-end rule}, which suggests that people tend to judge a temporal experience mainly according to its salient moments and the ending. Building on this intuition, we first transfer knowledge from image aesthetic assessment (IAA) to VAA by introducing a pretrained IAA head to produce frame-wise aesthetic priors, which serve as surrogate signals for identifying aesthetically salient moments and guiding \textit{peak-end rule}-based temporal aggregation. To further capture how a video evolves aesthetically over time, we design an aesthetic rhythm encoder that models temporal progression beyond isolated moments. Additionally, we refine the overall assessment through a dynamic gated fusion mechanism to improve robustness under distribution shift. Our method is built on a frozen vision transformer (ViT) and requires only a small number of trainable parameters, making it scalable and parameter-efficient. Extensive experiments on two existing VAA benchmarks, including in-domain evaluation on VADB and cross-domain testing on DIVIDE-3K, demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, affirming the value of psychologically grounded modeling for VAA. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/AMAP-ML/Peak-End-Net.

</details>


### [112] [PlumeQuant: Uncertainty-aware consistency assessment of methane plume masks and emission-rate estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13945)
*Parisa Masnadi Khiabani,Wolfgang Jentner,Alireza Rangrazjeddi,Michael C. Wimberly,Binbin Weng,David Ebert,Charles Nicholson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PlumeQuant工具，通过遗传算法集成和透明掩模重建，揭示了EMIT/Carbon Mapper甲烷羽流产品中存在的“等终性”问题——即已发布的标量量值（IME、羽流长度、排放率）无法唯一约束羽流边界，高置信核心区域仅占可能足迹包络的中位13%。方法在63条记录上验证，CM-like掩模对IME的中位差异为+0.72%，排放率为+0.16%，与参考掩模的中位IoU达0.843。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成像光谱仪提供的甲烷羽流产品（掩模、IME、长度、排放率及不确定性）在物理和算法上相互关联，但研究发现这些标量量值并不能唯一确定羽流边界——不同的掩模可能产生相同的标量值（等终性），这导致产品解析力不足，需要量化这种模糊性。

Method: 使用63条EMIT/Carbon Mapper羽流记录，采用遗传算法（GA）集成生成条件于已发布IME和羽流长度的掩模集合，显式刻画等终性；开发PlumeQuant工具，从分布式产品分量出发，依据既定约定重新计算IME、羽流长度、排放率及五项不确定性，并评估四种掩模表示（参考掩模、CM-like掩模、GA集成掩模、专家编辑掩模）。CM-like掩模在无参考掩模和已发布量值的情况下生成，参数在44条场景不相交的开发集上一次固定。

Result: CM-like掩模与参考掩模相比：IME中位差异+0.72%，排放率+0.16%（平均绝对6.98%），中位IoU 0.843，不确定性尺度中位比1.01；留出集平均绝对误差：IME 7.6%，长度9.5%，排放率6.1%。GA集合显示高置信核心区域仅覆盖可能足迹包络的中位13%，弱羽流和低重叠羽流模糊性最大。

Conclusion: PlumeQuant提供的产品级一致性诊断（非独立验证）揭示了羽流产品中等终性的存在和程度，能够标记弱、偏移或模糊的羽流以供专家审查，从而提升产品的可信度和可解释性。

Abstract: Imaging spectrometers increasingly distribute source-resolved methane plume products in which the plume mask, integrated mass enhancement (IME), plume length, emission rate, and uncertainty are physically and algorithmically linked. Using 63 EMIT-derived Carbon Mapper plume records from 27 scenes, we show that these published scalar quantities do not uniquely constrain the plume boundary: substantially different yet plausible masks reproduce the same IME, plume length, and emission rate. Genetic-algorithm (GA) ensembles conditioned on the published IME and plume length make this equifinality explicit: the high-confidence core selected by nearly all target-consistent masks covers a median of 13% of the plausible footprint envelope, and ambiguity is largest for weak, low-overlap plumes. The diagnostics come from PlumeQuant, which recomputes IME, plume length, emission rate, and five-term uncertainty from distributed product components under stated conventions and evaluates four mask representations: the distributed reference mask, a transparent Carbon Mapper-informed analogue (CM-like), the GA ensemble, and optional expert edits. The CM-like mask is generated per plume without access to the reference mask or published quantities, with settings fixed once on a scene-disjoint 44-plume development split. It reproduced published IME with +0.72% median difference and emission rate with +0.16% (6.98% mean absolute), reached 0.843 median intersection-over-union against the reference masks, and matched the published uncertainty scale (median ratio 1.01). Holdout mean absolute errors were 7.6% (IME), 9.5% (length), and 6.1% (rate). These are product-level consistency diagnostics, not independent validation. They flag weak, offset, or ambiguous plumes for expert review.

</details>


### [113] [CF-Net: Conflict Fusion with Speaker Normalisation and Certainty Weighting for Ambivalence/Hesitancy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13976)
*Tung Hung Bui,Hong Hai Nguyen,Van Thong Huynh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CF-Net是一种深度多模态网络，通过显式建模跨模态不一致性来检测视频中的矛盾与犹豫情绪，在BAH数据集上取得了0.7364 Macro F1的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非约束视频中的矛盾与犹豫检测极具挑战性，因为目标信号本质模糊且通过细微的跨模态不一致性而非典型情感表达，现有方法难以有效捕捉这种微妙信号。

Method: CF-Net采用冻结的SigLIP2（视觉）、HuBERT（音频）和DistilBERT（文本）骨干网络编码三模态流，通过逐说话人归一化减少身份泄漏，提出ConflictFusion模块显式计算成对跨模态不一致性进行融合。训练采用确定性加权的焦点损失、流形混合和模态丢弃策略，并引入辅助确定性回归头利用模糊标注稳定边界样本的学习。

Result: 在BAH验证集上Macro F1为0.7155，在私有挑战测试集上Macro F1为0.7364（AP=0.7492）。

Conclusion: 显式建模跨模态不一致性可有效检测矛盾与犹豫情绪，确定性加权损失和辅助回归头能提升边界样本的学习稳定性，为模糊情感信号的识别提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Detecting ambivalence and hesitancy (AH) in unconstrained video is challenging because the target signal is inherently ambiguous and expressed through subtle cross-modal incongruence rather than prototypical affect. We present CF-Net, a deep multimodal network submitted to the 3rd Edition of the AH Video Recognition Challenge (ABAW 11th, ECCV 2026), targeting the BAH dataset. CF-Net encodes visual, audio, and transcript streams with frozen SigLIP2, HuBERT, and DistilBERT backbones, normalises backbone features per speaker to reduce identity leakage, and fuses them via a ConflictFusion module that explicitly computes pairwise cross-modal incongruence. Training combines certainty-weighted focal loss, manifold mixup, and modality dropout; an auxiliary certainty-regression head leverages ambiguity annotations to stabilise learning on genuinely borderline samples. CF-Net achieves a Macro F1 of 0.7155 on the BAH validation set and 0.7364 (AP = 0.7492) on the private challenge test set.

</details>


### [114] [Music-to-Dance Generation via Atomic Movements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13978)
*Xinhao Cai,Yixuan Sun,Minghang Zheng,Qingchao Chen,Xin Jin,Song-chun Zhu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构感知的框架，将编舞建模为原子动作序列，先通过大语言模型构建可解释的原子动作词汇表，再采用两阶段生成（原子动作规划+过渡感知生成），在节奏同步、结构连贯性和可控性上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐驱动舞蹈生成方法将运动视为连续信号，忽略了舞蹈的组合特性，导致生成舞蹈结构不连贯且难以控制。

Method: 1) 对大尺度舞蹈数据进行分割并聚类为原子动作组；2) 利用大语言模型对聚类进行语义重标和精炼，构建可解释的原子动作词汇表；3) 两阶段生成框架：原子动作规划阶段预测类型、时长和时机，形成符号化舞蹈规划；过渡感知生成阶段合成平滑连贯的运动。

Result: 该方法在结构连贯性、节奏对齐和感知自然度上显著优于现有基线，并提供了更好的可解释性和可控编辑能力。

Conclusion: 通过将舞蹈显式建模为原子动作序列，实现了结构更连贯、节奏更对齐、更自然且易于编辑的舞蹈生成。

Abstract: Music-driven dance generation aims to produce human motion that is both rhythmically synchronized and semantically consistent with music. While recent neural approaches have achieved impressive visual realism, they typically model motion as a continuous signal and neglect its compositional nature, making generated dances structurally incoherent and difficult to control. In this work, we introduce a structure-aware framework that models choreography as a sequence of atomic movements-semantically interpretable motion events that serve as the building blocks of dance. To construct this atomic movement vocabulary, we first segment large-scale dance data and cluster them into atomic movement groups. We then employ a large language model to semantically relabel and refine the clusters, yielding a set of interpretable and reusable atomic movements. Based on these atomic movement annotations, we design a two-stage generation framework that mirrors the human choreography process. In the atomic movement planning stage, the model predicts the type, duration, and timing of atomic movements conditioned on the input music, forming a symbolic dance allocation. In the completion stage, a transition-aware generator synthesizes smooth and stylistically coherent motion conditioned on the planned structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method produces dances with significantly improved structural coherence, rhythmic alignment, and perceptual naturalness compared to existing baselines, while providing enhanced interpretability and controllable editing through explicit structural representation.

</details>


### [115] [Screening Is Effective for Visual Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13983)
*Shunya Shimomura,Kazuhiro Hotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VisionScreen，将语言模型中的Screening机制扩展到视觉识别领域，通过基于查询-键相似度的绝对相关性评估和阈值过滤，显式排除低相关图像块，替代了传统ViT中基于softmax注意力的相对特征聚合方式，在图像分类任务上取得了优于ViT的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT中的自注意力机制使用softmax对图像块分配相对权重，导致难以独立评估各块之间的相关性。图像中常包含大量背景或冗余块，而自注意力无法显式排除这些不相关块，从而引入不必要的噪声信息到特征聚合中。

Method: 将Screening机制扩展到二维空间域：将图像块视为排列在二维网格上的token，基于查询-键相似度进行绝对相关性估计，通过阈值化显式排除低相关性的图像块，使得每个图像块能够选择性聚合内容相关且空间相关的块，而不依赖于块间的竞争机制。

Result: 在图像分类基准测试上，VisionScreen优于传统的Vision Transformer (ViT)。

Conclusion: Screening机制对于视觉识别任务是有效的，为基于softmax注意力的相对特征聚合提供了一种替代方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformer (ViT) has been widely used as a powerful framework for modeling global dependencies among image patches. However, its core component, self-attention assigns softmax-normalized relative weights to all patches, making it difficult to evaluate the relevance between patches independently. In visual recognition, images often contain many background or redundant patches, yet self-attention cannot explicitly reject such irrelevant patches, which may introduce unnecessary information into feature aggregation. To address this limitation, Screening has been proposed in the field of language modeling, where the relevance of each token is independently evaluated based on query-key similarity and low-relevance tokens are explicitly excluded through thresholding. In this work, we propose VisionScreen, a new vision model that extends Screening mechanism to visual recognition. VisionScreen treats image patches as tokens arranged on a two-dimensional grid and extends absolute relevance estimation based on query-key similarity to the two-dimensional spatial domain. This allows each patch to selectively aggregate only content-wise and spatially relevant patches without relying on competition among patches. Experiments on image classification benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms conventional ViT. These results suggest that Screening can be effective for visual recognition, offering an alternative to relative feature aggregation based on softmax attention.

</details>


### [116] [Task-Specific Feature Fusion Method for Multi-Task Affective Behavior Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13986)
*Jiajun Sun,Zhe Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对ABAW11多任务情感行为分析挑战，提出了一种任务自适应特征融合方法。研究使用DINOv2 ViT-L和DINOv3 ConvNeXt-base两个预训练视觉骨干在外部面部图像上微调后冻结，提取互补帧级特征。系统性地比较了多种预测头、融合机制和校准策略，最终为每个任务选择特定的融合与预测方案，在验证集上取得EXPR宏F1=0.4222、AU宏F1=0.5402、VA CCC均值=0.6717，总分1.6341，证明了冻结视觉特征的任务自适应融合是一种简单有效的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABAW11多任务学习挑战要求一个统一系统同时预测效价-唤醒度、分类表情和面部动作单元。虽然这些任务在面部行为上自然相关，但验证实验表明它们需要不同的视觉特征、时序处理策略、融合机制和校准程序。现有方法强迫所有任务共享单一架构，无法充分适应各任务的差异性需求。

Method: 1) 使用两个预训练视觉骨干(DINOv2 ViT-L和DINOv3 ConvNeXt-base)在外部表情数据集上微调后冻结，提取互补帧级特征。2) 系统比较帧级预测头、时序卷积头、事后时序平滑、LightGBM模型、特征拼接、门控融合、残差融合、晚期logit融合、阈值校准和共享MTL结构等多种策略。3) 最终为每个任务选择特定的融合与预测方案，而非强制所有任务共享同一架构。

Result: 在ABAW11验证集上，EXPR宏F1=0.4222，AU宏F1=0.5402，VA CCC均值=0.6717，总体验证分数1.6341。

Conclusion: 任务自适应融合冻结视觉特征是一种简单有效的ABAW风格多任务情感行为分析策略，为不同任务选择特定架构优于强制统一架构的方法。

Abstract: The 11th Affective Behavior Analysis in-the-wild (ABAW11) Multi-Task Learning Challenge requires a unified system to predict valence-arousal, categorical expressions, and facial action units from the official s-Aff-Wild2 images. Although these tasks are naturally related through facial behavior, our validation experiments show that they benefit from different visual features, temporal processing strategies, fusion mechanisms, and calibration procedures. In this paper, we study task-adaptive feature fusion for ABAW11 multi-task affective behavior analysis. We first adapt two pretrained visual backbones, DINOv2 ViT-L and DINOv3 ConvNeXt-base, on an external expression-oriented facial image set and then freeze them to extract complementary frame-level features from the official ABAW11 data. On top of these frozen features, we systematically compare frame-level prediction heads, temporal convolutional heads, post-hoc temporal smoothing, LightGBM models, feature concatenation, gated fusion, residual fusion, late logit fusion, threshold calibration, and shared MTL structures. The final system selects task-specific fusion and prediction strategies rather than forcing all tasks to share a single architecture. On the ABAW11 validation set, the selected system achieves an EXPR macro-F1 of 0.4222, an AU macro-F1 of 0.5402, and a mean VA CCC of 0.6717, resulting in an overall validation score of 1.6341. The results suggest that task-adaptive fusion of frozen visual features is a simple and effective strategy for ABAW-style multi-task affective behavior analysis.

</details>


### [117] [M$^\text{4}$World: A Multi-view Multimodal Driving World Model for Interactive Object Manipulation and Minute-long Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14005)
*Ke Cheng,Hanqiao Ye,Lei Shi,Yahui Liu,Yunhan Shen,Jingtao Dong,Zhenke Wang,Wenxuan Ao,Weixiang Xu,Kaining Huang,Shuhan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: M⁴World是一个多视图、多模态生成式驾驶世界模型，支持可交互的物体操控和分钟级稳定流式生成，通过灵活的条件控制接口实现细粒度物体控制，并通过多阶段训练框架在仅4步去噪中完成在线因果生成，同时引入少样本后训练和视觉参考条件生成模型以支持长尾可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界生成方法在物体级可控性和长时域稳定性方面存在局限，缺乏对单个物体空间布局和视觉外观的精细控制能力，且难以实现分钟级的长时稳定流式生成。

Method: 1) 灵活的条件控制接口，支持对单个物体的空间布局和视觉外观进行显式控制；2) 多阶段训练框架，仅需4步去噪即可实现在线因果生成，保持扩展 rollout 中一致的世界动态；3) 少样本后训练和视觉参考条件生成模型，在不损失通用生成能力的前提下实现长尾定制化。

Result: M⁴World在生成质量、精确可控性和分钟级稳定流式生成方面均表现优异，并通过自动化VLM评估流水线验证了场景级条件遵循、视图级物体可控性和跨视图物体一致性。

Conclusion: M⁴World通过可控、可扩展的驾驶仿真，在下游的长尾增强和场景编辑任务中展现出巨大潜力。

Abstract: Driving-world generation has emerged as a core capability for scalable autonomous-driving simulation, yet existing methods remain limited in object-level controllability and long-horizon stability. We present M$^\text{4}$World, a Multi-view and Multimodal generative driving world model that synthesizes future surround-view video streams and synchronized LiDAR scans while supporting interactive object Manipulation and stable Minute-long streaming. Fine-grained object manipulation is realized through a flexible conditioning interface that supports explicit control over both the spatial layout and visual appearance of individual objects. Stable minute-long streaming, on the other hand, is achieved through a multi-stage training framework that enables online causal generation in only four denoising steps while maintaining coherent world dynamics throughout extended rollouts. Building on these components, we introduce an efficient few-clip post-training as well as a suite of visual reference-conditioned generation models, preserving general generation ability while allowing rare-case customization for long-tail controllability. To assess controllability beyond realism, we further introduce an automated VLM-based judging pipeline that evaluates scene-level condition adherence, view-wise object controllability, and cross-view object consistency. Comprehensive experiments show that M$^\text{4}$World consistently delivers high generation quality, precise controllability, and stable minute-long streaming. Together with downstream long-tail augmentation and scene editing, these results demonstrate the potential of M$^\text{4}$World for controllable, scalable driving simulation.

</details>


### [118] [Multi-Expert Routing for Multi-Domain Low-Resource OCR: A Manchu Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14041)
*Zhan Chen,Jiqiao Ma,Chih-wen Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多专家路由系统，利用迭代微调过程中的检查点作为领域专家，通过轻量级页面级图像分类器按视觉风格（楷书、行书、奏折半草书）分派满文OCR任务。在三个冻结测试集上，路由系统以两位小数精度匹配每个风格的选定专家，页面级领域准确率达99.3%，且两个专家并非针对其最终领域专门训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史满文OCR面临多种视觉上截然不同的书写风格（楷书、行书、奏折半草书），且标注数据有限，现有方法难以在不增加大量标注数据的情况下统一处理这些风格。

Method: 采用多专家系统，将迭代微调过程中的检查点复用作领域专家，并训练一个轻量级页面级图像分类器来按视觉风格路由页面。当检查点池中缺少合适的专家时，额外训练该领域的专家。

Result: 路由系统在三个冻结测试集上以两位小数精度匹配每个风格的选定专家：楷书CER 0.30%、奏折1.57%、行书4.83%；路由器页面级领域准确率99.3%，与领域标签oracle精度一致。

Conclusion: 该路由方法无需为每个风格专门训练专家即可有效匹配，仅行书专家是针对其领域训练的；路由器设计具有可重复性，提供了评估协议和逐页预测。

Abstract: Historical Manchu OCR must accommodate various visually distinct writing styles, including regular script, running script, and the semi-cursive chancery hand used in palace memorials, despite limited labeled data. We study a multi-expert system that reuses checkpoints from an iterative fine-tuning process as domain specialists and uses a lightweight page-level image classifier to dispatch pages by visual style. When the checkpoint pool lacks a suitable specialist, we train an additional expert for that domain. On three frozen test sets, the routed system matches the selected specialist for each style at two-decimal precision: 0.30 percent CER on regular script, 1.57 percent on memorials, and 4.83 percent on running script. The router achieves 99.3 percent page-level domain accuracy and matches the domain-label oracle at the same precision. Two of the three selected specialists were not trained specifically for their final domain; only the running-script expert was trained with that domain as its target. We report the evaluation protocol, router design, and per-page predictions to make the comparison reproducible.

</details>


### [119] [From Pixels to States: Rethinking Interactive World Models as Game Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14076)
*Zhen Li,Zian Meng,Shuwei Shi,Mingliang Zhai,Jiaming Tan,Chuanhao Li,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以"动作-状态-观测"循环为组织框架，系统分析了交互式游戏世界建模在四个维度上的方法与挑战，并提出了包含90小时《黑神话：悟空》游戏数据的可扩展数据引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能根据玩家动作连贯响应的交互式游戏世界是计算机图形学、游戏和AI的共同目标。尽管视频生成模型被视为潜在的新一代游戏引擎，但实现真正的交互式游戏世界仍需满足：按规则演化的交互结果、长时间跨度的持久化后果以及实时生成能力。

Method: 以传统游戏引擎的"动作→状态更新→观测渲染"循环为组织视角，沿四个维度（玩家动作控制、游戏状态动态、状态-观测持久性、实时交互生成）系统分析现有方法，并按代表性家族分组讨论各自的优势与权衡。同时，为《黑神话：悟空》构建了包含90小时游戏数据、帧级对齐的玩家动作、真实游戏状态和视觉观测及其结构和语义标注的可扩展数据引擎。

Result: 从四个维度梳理了交互式游戏世界建模的现有方法家族及其优劣，并提供了一个大规模、标注丰富的数据集资源，以支持状态感知的游戏世界建模研究。

Conclusion: 本文为交互式游戏世界建模提供了清晰的领域全景图，有助于推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: Building interactive worlds that respond coherently to player actions has long been a shared goal of computer graphics, games, and artificial intelligence. Recent video generative models provide a data-driven route toward this goal by predicting future observations conditioned on user actions, and are increasingly regarded as potential next-generation game engines. Realizing a genuinely interactive game world, however, requires interaction outcomes that follow rules over evolving game conditions, consequences that persist over long horizons, and a generation loop that operates in real time. Conventional game engines realize these properties through a recurrent action-state-observation loop, in which player actions update an explicit game state according to predefined rules and observations are rendered from the resulting state. Taking this loop as an organizing lens, this paper examines interactive game world modeling along four dimensions: player action control, game state dynamics, state-observation persistence, and real-time interactive generation. For each dimension, we start from the capabilities required by an interactive game world, group existing approaches into representative families, and discuss the strengths and trade-offs of each family. Complementing this analysis, we present a scalable data engine for Black Myth: Wukong that collects over 90 hours of gameplay with frame-aligned player actions, ground-truth game states, and visual observations, together with structured and semantic annotations, as a resource for state-aware game world modeling. We hope this paper offers a clear picture of where the field stands and fosters progress toward interactive game worlds.

</details>


### [120] [VideoRAE: Taming Video Foundation Models for Generative Modeling via Representation Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14088)
*Zhihao Xie,Junfeng Wu,Xinting Hu,Junchao Huang,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoRAE，利用冻结的视频基础模型（如V-JEPA 2、VideoMAEv2）的多尺度层次特征，通过轻量级1D自注意力投影器将其压缩为紧凑的、可重建且适合生成的视频潜在表示。VideoRAE支持连续潜在（用于DiT）和离散token（用于自回归模型），并通过局部-全局表示对齐目标提升语义保持。实验表明其重建质量优异，在UCF-101上取得SOTA，收敛速度比基线快约5倍，并在2B规模文生视频研究中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D-VAE主要针对像素级重建优化，限制了潜在空间对语义和时空结构的捕获能力。同时，已有视频基础模型虽具备强理解能力，但其冻结表示能否转化为紧凑、可重建且适合生成的视频潜在表示尚未被探索。

Method: VideoRAE包含三部分：(1) 冻结的视频基础编码器提取多尺度层次特征；(2) 轻量级1D自注意力投影器进行压缩；(3) 支持连续潜在（用于DiT）和通过多码书高维量化的离散token（用于自回归模型）。解码时采用局部-全局表示对齐目标与冻结的VFM教师进行语义保持，无需KL正则化。

Result: 在UCF-101上，VideoRAE在AR和DiT生成器下分别取得40和93的gFVD，达到SOTA；收敛速度比竞争基线快约5倍。在2B规模文生视频实验中，替换LTX-VAE为VideoRAE在可比设置下实现更快收敛。

Conclusion: 冻结的视频基础模型表示可以作为通用且适合生成的视频潜在表示，VideoRAE验证了该范式的可行性和高效性。

Abstract: Video generative models commonly rely on latent spaces learned by 3D Variational Autoencoders (3D-VAEs). However, conventional 3D-VAEs are mainly optimized for pixel-level reconstruction, which can limit the semantic and spatio-temporal structure captured by their latents. Meanwhile, Video Foundation Models (VFMs) such as V-JEPA 2 and VideoMAEv2 show strong video understanding capabilities, yet whether their frozen representations can be transformed into compact, reconstruction-capable, and generation-friendly video latents remains largely unexplored. We answer this question with VideoRAE, a representation autoencoder that leverages multi-scale hierarchical features from a frozen video foundation encoder and compresses them with a lightweight 1D self-attention projector. VideoRAE supports both continuous latents for Diffusion Transformers and discrete tokens for autoregressive models via multi-codebook high-dimensional quantization. During decoding, a local-and-global representation alignment objective with the frozen VFM teacher improves semantic preservation and enables training without KL regularization. Experiments show that VideoRAE achieves strong reconstruction in both continuous and discrete regimes. On UCF-101, it obtains state-of-the-art class-to-video gFVDs of 40 and 93 with AR and DiT generators, respectively, while converging approximately 5x faster than competing autoencoder baselines. In a controlled 2B-scale text-to-video study, replacing LTX-VAE with VideoRAE leads to faster convergence under comparable settings. These results validate frozen VFM representations as versatile and generation-friendly video latents. The model and code will be released on https://zhxie0117.github.io/VideoRAE.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [The Entanglement Wall: Activation-Space Probes as Risk Detectors, Not Context Adjudicators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13075)
*Dominik Schwarz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了残差流探针能否区分有害请求与表面匹配的良性对照。在三个7-8B模型家族中，激活传感器在分类法选定集上阻断了95.5-97.7%的合规攻击，在XSTest上阻断59.6-68.4%。完全分离的审计重建了近天花板源对比AUROC（0.996-0.999），但固定迁移到匹配对较弱。最终结论是这些激活分数表现为广泛风险检测器，而非独立的上下文裁决器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究残差流探针是否能在不改变主题或表面形式的情况下，区分有害请求与表面匹配的良性对照，验证其作为上下文安全裁决器的可行性。

Method: 使用残差流探针（激活传感器）对三个7-8B模型家族进行测试，评估其在分类法选定攻击集、XSTest提示集上的阻断率，并进行完全分离审计、固定迁移测试，同时设置泄漏、保留和置换对照，最后在24B/32B模型上进行扩展验证。

Result: 激活传感器在分类法选定集上阻断95.5-97.7%合规攻击，XSTest上阻断59.6-68.4%；完全分离审计AUROC达0.996-0.999，但固定迁移较弱（Twin-n70上0.656-0.819，Twin-n163上0.590-0.690）；对Twin-n163，无轴达到设定阈值；对训练的分类器在类别和生成批次保留下变弱，在95%内TPR下XSTest误报率达79.6-100%。

Conclusion: 残差流探针的激活分数表现为广泛风险检测器，而非独立的上下文裁决器，无法作为可靠的细粒度有害请求识别工具。

Abstract: Context can change whether a request is harmful without changing its topic or surface form. We ask whether residual-stream probes distinguish harmful requests from surface-matched benign controls at a useful operating point. Across three 7-8B model families, an activation sensor blocks 95.5-97.7 percent of judge-classified compliant attacks in a taxonomy-selected set. It also blocks 59.6-68.4 percent of XSTest prompts. A fully disjoint audit reconstructs near-ceiling source-contrast AUROC (0.996-0.999), but fixed transfer to matched pairs is weaker: 0.656-0.819 on the guard-selected Twin-n70 subset and 0.590-0.690 on the full Twin-n163 cohort. We test ten axes on the reference family and seven across all families with leakage, hold-out, and permutation controls. On Twin-n163, no axis evaluated without direct pair-boundary fitting reaches the specified numerical threshold. Requiring persistence on that full cohort was added at analysis time. A separately specified 24B/32B extension gives the same result. Pair-trained classifiers weaken under category and generation-batch hold-out and false-block 79.6-100 percent of XSTest at 95 percent in-corpus TPR. At the tested read points, these activation scores behave as broad-risk detectors rather than standalone context adjudicators.

</details>


### [122] [Operational Evidence Gaps for LLMs in Fraud Detection and Trust-and-Safety Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13078)
*Keyur Gabani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 这篇论文通过欺诈优先的视角调查了LLM在信任与安全（欺诈检测、内容审核等）工作流中的部署证据。作者编码了49篇操作相关文献，发现证据分布不均衡：欺诈相关论文缺乏延迟、成本和校准等关键部署指标，而内容审核论文包含了更多关于延迟、成本、治理和公平性的公开证据。论文提出了FORTE角色-证据组织框架和一个最低部署证据清单，并指出了支持部署主张所需的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献主要将LLM作为独立模型进行评估，较少关注它们在操作化流水线中作为组件的行为表现。在延迟、成本、升级机制、人工复核和对抗性风险等实际约束下，缺乏将LLM部署到实时工作流中的实证依据。

Method: 采用欺诈优先的调查方法，编码49篇操作相关文献（18篇欺诈检测、14篇内容审核、17篇跨领域），辅以15篇上下文参考文献。这些来源包括系统、基准测试、框架和部署相关调查，而非49个实际生产部署。

Result: 发现证据严重失衡：欺诈与调查相关的18篇文献中，没有一篇报告每次决策的延迟、每次决策的美元成本或校准证据；多数仅报告离线任务性能、检索增益或案例研究准确率。相比之下，内容审核文献包含了更多关于延迟、成本、治理和公平性的明确公开证据。

Conclusion: 贡献了FORTE角色-证据组织框架（将LLM定位为分类器、检索接口、解释生成器、审核助手、智能体、特征提取器或升级组件），以及包含延迟预算、每次决策成本、决策阈值、解释完整性和对抗压力的最低部署证据清单。提出了支持LLM在欺诈和信任安全领域部署主张所需的研究议程。

Abstract: LLMs are now proposed for fraud detection, scam investigation, content moderation, and other trust-and-safety workflows. Much of the public literature still evaluates them as models, with less attention to their behavior as components in operational pipelines. This creates a practical evidence question: what would justify placing an LLM inside a live workflow with latency, cost, escalation, human-review, and adversarial-risk constraints?
  We address this question through a fraud-first survey of deployment evidence. We code 49 operationally relevant sources on LLM use in fraud detection, investigation support, content moderation, and cross-cutting robustness (18 fraud, 14 moderation, 17 cross-cutting), supplemented by 15 contextual references that establish the survey boundaries. These sources include systems, benchmarks, frameworks, and deployment-relevant surveys, not 49 production deployments.
  The main finding is an evidence imbalance. Fraud supplies the largest task-specific portion of the coded corpus. The moderation papers, however, include more explicit public evidence on latency, cost, governance, and fairness. Among the 18 fraud and investigation sources, none report clean per-decision latency, per-decision dollar cost, or calibration evidence; most report offline task performance, retrieval gains, or case-study accuracy instead.
  The survey contributes a role-and-evidence organizing frame, FORTE, for locating LLMs as classifiers, retrieval interfaces, explanation generators, reviewer assistants, agents, feature extractors, or escalation components. It also contributes a minimum deployment-evidence checklist covering latency budget, cost per decision, decision threshold, explanation integrity, and adversarial pressure. The resulting agenda identifies studies needed to support deployment claims for LLM-based fraud and trust-and-safety work.

</details>


### [123] [SingGuard-NSFA: Extensible Guardrails for Agentic AI via Generative Reasoning and Real-Time Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13081)
*SingGuard Team*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: nsfaguard是一个针对智能体AI系统的防护框架，基于NSFA分类法（涵盖185种风险变体，按CIA三元组分层组织），构建了包含133种语言、超过93K个样本的基准测试集，并提出了结合SFT生成式推理（可解释离线审计）和判别式分类头（实时检测，约50ms）的双模式方法。发布的0.8B至9B四个模型在目的构建基准上均达到≥94% F1，超越最强竞品6-12个绝对百分点，9B模型在跨源评估中达到91.29% F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI系统面临多种操作威胁（如提示注入、敏感信息提取、恶意代码请求、危险工具滥用和资源耗尽），现有防护措施缺乏系统化的分类体系和全面的基准测试，亟需一个统一、可扩展的防护框架来检测和防御这些威胁。

Method: 提出NSFA分类法，将185种风险变体按CIA三元组分层组织，并与三项OWASP指南交叉验证。构建包含133种语言、93K+目的构建样本和3,435个跨源样本的基准。开发双模式检测方法：SFT生成式推理用于可解释离线审计，判别式分类头（基于冻结骨干网络）用于实时检测（约50ms延迟）。发布0.8B、2B、4B和9B四个参数规模的模型。

Result: 四个模型在目的构建基准上均达到≥94% F1，超越最强竞品6-12个绝对百分点。9B模型在跨源评估中达到91.29% F1，精度-召回权衡更均衡。消融实验表明分类头可使防护模型获得超出原始范围的检测能力，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: nsfaguard展示了该方法的可扩展性和作为即插即用增强模块的通用性，证明了分类头架构能有效扩展防护模型的风险检测能力。

Abstract: We present nsfaguard, a guardrail framework for securing agentic AI systems against operational threats, such as prompt injection, sensitive information extraction, malicious code requests, dangerous tool misuse, and resource exhaustion. We first introduce the NSFA taxonomy, which organizes 185 risk variants into a CIA-triad-grounded hierarchy and is cross-validated against three well-established OWASP guidelines. Based on this taxonomy, we construct a benchmark suite spanning 133 languages, comprising over 93K purpose-built samples targeting both user queries and agent responses, along with 3,435 cross-source samples adapted from five public agent-security datasets. To detect these operational threats in practice, we develop a dual-mode approach combining SFT-based generative reasoning for interpretable offline auditing with discriminative classification heads on the frozen backbone, enabling real-time detection at approximately 50,ms. We release four models with 0.8B, 2B, 4B, and 9B parameters, all achieving $\geq$94% F1 on purpose-built benchmarks and surpassing the strongest competing guardrails by 6 to 12 absolute points. On cross-source evaluation, the 9B model attains 91.29% F1 with a more balanced precision--recall trade-off. Moreover, ablation experiments show that classification heads can equip a guardrail with risk detection capabilities beyond its original scope and achieve state-of-the-art performance. These results demonstrate the extensibility of the approach and its generality as a plug-in enhancement.

</details>


### [124] [Beyond AI-Generated Labels: Watermarking, Co-Creation, and Conflation of AI-Generation with Disinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13082)
*Federico Germani,Giovanni Spitale*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证了将水印机制（尤其是可见的AI生成标签）应用于区分AI与人类内容存在概念和实践上的缺陷，并提出以过程透明度和信息素养作为更有效的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑将水印机制（通常被视为区分AI生成与人类生成内容的直接解决方案）扩展到关注信息准确性、意图和欺骗性潜力的领域是否合理，指出当前方法忽视了认知和伦理层面的挑战。

Method: 通过概念分析和批判性论证，指出不可见水印仅编码模型来源，可见AI标签将复杂创作过程简化为误导性的二元分类且无法提供真实性信息，并提出以过程透明度和信息素养为核心的替代方案。

Result: 发现可见的AI生成标签可能污名化生成式工具的合法使用，同时鼓励对未标记内容的错误信任；过程透明度和信息素养能更有效地应对AI生成虚假信息的认知和伦理维度。

Conclusion: 应将作者身份重新理解为一个透明的人类实践过程，而非二元化的机器参与指标；过程透明度和信息素养是比可见水印标签更有效的应对AI虚假信息的方法。

Abstract: Watermarking is often presented as a straightforward solution for distinguishing AI-generated from human-generated content, enabling platforms and regulators to trace synthetic content and detect AI-generated outputs at scale. This paper examines whether such mechanisms meaningfully address the epistemic and ethical challenges that arise in domains where the central concern is not the automation of content production, but the accuracy, intent, and deceptive potential of messages. We argue that extending watermark-based approaches to these settings is conceptually and practically misguided. Invisible watermarking encodes only model origin; when operationalized into visible AI-generated labels, it reduces complex creative processes to a misleading binary and provides no information about truthfulness. Such labels may stigmatize legitimate uses of generative tools while encouraging misplaced trust in unmarked content. Here we propose an alternative approach centered on process transparency and information literacy. We argue that these measures address the epistemic and ethical dimensions of AI-generated disinformation more effectively than visible watermark labels, reframing authorship as a transparent human practice rather than a binary indicator of machine involvement.

</details>


### [125] [Phantom Guardrails: When Self-Improving Agent Harnesses Fix Failures That Never Happened](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13083)
*Su Wang,Pin Qian,Yifan Lin,Jingzhou Xu,Yihang Chen,Xiaochong Jiang,Lifei Liu,Haoran Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现自改进AI代理在优化过程中会"幻觉"出从未发生的失败，并据此创建虚假的防护栏（phantom guardrail）。通过确定性微实验室（Counterfactual Fabrication Lab）验证，当三个条件同时满足时（规则形状的模式、开放规则集、预设失败指令），这种虚构行为在15/60的运行中出现，而移除任一条件则完全消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自改进AI代理研究关注其从真实错误中学习的能力，但忽略了代理可能编造从未发生的错误并进行修复的风险。作者希望揭示这种失败模式，以确保自改进系统的安全性和可靠性。

Method: 构建Counterfactual Fabrication Lab（确定性微实验室）：已知正确动作为"什么都不做"；植入一个证明永远不会发生的失败类别的防护栏；仅展示合法片段；使用字节精确预言机检查每个引用的违规。在单次和循环添加条件下测试LLM提议者的行为。

Result: 在包含无害但类似游戏规则模式的合法输入上，提议者在15/60轮次中虚构出失败并启用不存在的规则防护栏（特征输入上0/60）。循环添加条件下，虚构出的防护栏会持续存在。移除三个条件中任意一个（规则形状模式、开放规则集、预设失败指令），虚构行为完全消失。

Conclusion: 自改进AI代理可能产生"幻影防护栏"（phantom guardrail）——修复从未发生过的失败，这种行为既不是奖励黑客也不是过度拒绝，而是由特定条件组合触发的结构化虚构。这种故障在仅接受添加的循环中会自我维持，对系统安全性构成隐蔽威胁。

Abstract: Self-improving AI agents are designed to learn from their mistakes. We show they can also hallucinate mistakes that never happened. We study this failure mode in automated harness optimization, where an LLM-based proposer edits an agent's scaffold, including prompts, parsers, filters, validators and guardrails, to eliminate observed failures. But this process rarely asks first: was there a real failure to fix? We introduce the Counterfactual Fabrication Lab, a deterministic micro-lab where the correct action is known: do nothing. The lab plants a candidate guardrail for a failure class that provably never occurs, presents only legal episodes, and uses a byte-exact oracle to check every cited violation. The proposer behaves as expected on real violations and abstains on featureless legal input. Yet when the legal input contains a harmless pattern resembling a familiar game rule, it invents a failure: in 15/60 runs, versus 0/60 on featureless input, it enables the nonexistent-rule guardrail and cites a violation the oracle refutes. The effect is structured, not indiscriminate. In single-shot proposals it appears only when three conditions coincide: a rule-shaped pattern, an open-ended rule set and an instruction that presupposes failures. Removing any of these conditions eliminates the fabrication. Because the invented guardrail changes no true outcome and cannot improve an already-perfect suppression score, the phenomenon is neither reward hacking nor over-refusal. It is a phantom guardrail: a fix for a failure that never happened, invisible to suppression-only acceptance. Inside an add-only accept loop it re-enters even without the failure-presupposing instruction, the loop's keep-adding role supplying the demand the instruction supplied in single shot, and once in it stays. We present the Counterfactual Fabrication Lab for measuring fabricated failures in self-improving agent harnesses.

</details>


### [126] [Baselines Before Architecture: Evaluating Coding Agents for Autonomous Penetration Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13085)
*Ananda Dhakal,Krish Neupane,Aarjan Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过控制变量实验发现，默认编码CLI智能体在XBOW基准测试中已能解决大部分任务，专用渗透测试框架带来的性能提升有限，且同一框架下模型升级带来的增益显著。因此，未来评估应将模型匹配的普通智能体作为基线，避免将基准分数提升简单归因于架构设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主渗透测试论文多同时改变架构和底层模型，导致难以区分性能提升来自安全框架（harness）还是底层模型本身。本文旨在通过控制变量实验厘清这一混淆因素。

Method: 在104任务的XBOW基准上，以默认编码CLI智能体作为普通智能体基线。第一阶段：使用相同GPT-5模型、预算、目标接口和评分规则，运行Codex、OpenCode和Pi，确定同模型最强基线并测试安全提示变体效果；第二阶段：将默认Codex框架与已发表的MAPTA和PentestGPT V2结果进行模型匹配对比；第三阶段：在相同框架下用GPT-5.2和GPT-5.5重复实验，测量模型规模效应。

Result: 专用安全框架可带来可测量的基准提升并改善成本效率，但普通编码智能体已能解决大部分基准任务；多次普通智能体运行结果的并集覆盖率可达到或超过部分已发表架构的分数；同一框架下更新模型显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 未来评估应报告模型匹配的普通智能体基线，在将基准增益归因于架构设计之前需排除模型本身的贡献。

Abstract: Recent autonomous penetration testing papers report high benchmark scores while adding multi-component security harnesses around frontier LLMs. Because these systems often change both architecture and backbone model, it is difficult to tell how much performance comes from the harness rather than from the underlying model.
  This paper presents a controlled study on the 104-task XBOW benchmark using default coding CLI agents as plain-agent baselines. We first run Codex, OpenCode, and Pi with the same GPT-5 model, budget, target interface, and scoring rule. This phase identifies the strongest same-model baseline and tests whether security-specific prompt variants improve its observed score. We then compare the default Codex scaffold with published MAPTA and PentestGPT V2 results under the closest available model matches. Finally, we repeat the plain-agent experiment with GPT-5.2 and GPT-5.5 to measure model scaling inside the same scaffold.
  The results show a mixed but practical picture. Specialised harnesses can add measurable benchmark lift and may improve cost efficiency, but plain coding agents already solve a large share of the benchmark; repeated plain-agent runs can match or exceed some published architecture scores in union coverage, and newer models substantially improve the same scaffold. Future evaluations should report model-matched plain-agent baselines before attributing benchmark gains to architecture design alone.

</details>


### [127] [GDM AI Control Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13087)
*Mary Phuong,Erik Jenner,Laurent Simon,Lewis Ho,Rohin Shah,Sebastian Farquhar,Scott Coull*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了GDM AI控制路线图(v0.1)，这是首个针对潜在恶意AI的内部安全蓝图。该路线图通过威胁建模(TRAIT&R分类法)、基于能力的缓解措施(四个检测层级D1-D4和三个预防响应层级R1-R3)以及15个分层防御实践，为前沿AI公司构建多层次安全防御体系提供了系统性方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理在AI研发、网络防御和科学发现等领域加速应用，传统的完美对齐假设不可靠。需要重新思考安全策略，构建多层防御体系来应对可能未对齐的AI代理带来的潜在威胁。

Method: 1) 威胁建模：采用保守假设，引入TRAIT&R分类法（基于MITRE ATT&CK框架），系统化分类AI对手可能的战术和技术；2) 基于能力的缓解措施：将防御措施与模型能力演进挂钩，定义D1-D4检测层级和R1-R3预防响应层级；3) 提出15个具体、分层级的防御实践，从低成本干预到高级安全保障。

Result: 形成了首个针对内部部署中潜在恶意AI的安全蓝图，包含完整的威胁分类体系、能力驱动的防御层级框架以及15个实用的分层缓解措施。

Conclusion: AI控制是一个新兴领域，实施这些缓解措施需要在安全性与开发者速度之间权衡。该路线图将随着经验积累和领域发展而持续演进。

Abstract: AI agents are rapidly accelerating work at frontier AI companies, helping with AI R&D, cyber-defence, and advancing scientific discoveries. As these agents become more tightly integrated into our systems, unlocking their full potential requires rethinking how we do security. We should not assume that AI agents are always perfectly aligned, but should instead build in multiple layers of defence. We present the GDM AI Control Roadmap (v0.1) -- a first-of-its-kind blueprint for internal security against potentially misaligned AI. This report provides:
  * Threat modelling: We adopt a conservative approach to threat modelling and assume a hypothetical AI adversary pursuing undesirable goals in internal deployment. We introduce TRAIT&R, a taxonomy of tactics and techniques available to such a hypothetical AI adversary, building on the established security framework MITRE ATT&CK.
  * Capability-based mitigation: Because controlling more capable models requires more costly interventions, we link specific defensive measures to evolving model capabilities (such as the ability to reason opaquely or execute complex cyberattacks). As models get more powerful, our defences should escalate accordingly. We outline four Detection tiers (D1-D4) and three Prevention and Response tiers (R1-R3).
  * A portfolio of practical defences: We suggest 15 concrete, tiered mitigations. These range from low-cost interventions for current models (e.g., chain-of-thought monitoring, asynchronous alerts) to advanced safeguards for future models (e.g., real-time access control, system-level anomaly detection, internal activations monitoring, and shutdown infrastructure).
  AI control is a nascent field, and implementing these mitigations requires navigating difficult trade-offs between security and developer velocity. We expect the roadmap to evolve as we gain more experience and as the field in turn evolves.

</details>


### [128] [Securing LLMs in the Wild: Privacy and Security Challenges at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13088)
*Ren-Yi Huang,Mingchen Li,Dumindu Samaraweera,Morris Chang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"安全-效率悖论"概念，系统分析了LLM在边缘/本地部署时因资源受限而采取的优化（量化、剪枝、模型分区、参数高效微调）所引入的新安全与隐私风险，并构建了以三大架构约束（内存墙、二次方墙、计算墙）为核心的部署中心化分类体系，提出了统一约束模型、安全运营效率评分（SOES）及实用决策流程与缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正向边缘和本地部署迁移，以解决数据主权、合规性、延迟和第三方依赖等问题；然而边缘设备的有限算力和内存迫使采用激进的优化手段，这些优化可能削弱模型安全性、引入新攻击面或增加隐私风险，现有研究缺乏对这一矛盾的系统性分析框架。

Method: 提出部署中心化分类体系，围绕三大架构约束（Memory Wall、Quadratic Wall、Compute Wall）建模；推导统一约束模型量化不安全优化何时变得不可避免；构建SOES综合指标平衡任务准确率、越狱抵抗和隐私与能耗、内存、延迟；给出实用决策流程和针对性缓解措施。

Result: 揭示了压缩降低安全对齐、分区推理使重建攻击成为可能、持续本地微调导致隐私泄漏与模型漂移等风险；通过统一约束模型将每类架构墙与特定攻击面关联；SOES指标使从业者可在真实硬件限制下配置边缘LLM。

Conclusion: 提供了一个安全、隐私与效率协同设计的框架，为保障边缘原生智能系统的安全性奠定基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are rapidly moving from research settings into the wild, deployed on enterprise infrastructure, personal devices, and edge platforms. While cloud deployments offer scalable compute, concerns over data sovereignty, compliance, latency, and third-party dependence are driving organizations toward edge and on-premise LLMs. This shift introduces new security and privacy challenges: limited compute and memory force aggressive optimizations, including quantization, pruning, model partitioning, and parameter-efficient adaptation, each of which can introduce vulnerabilities and reshape the threat landscape. We describe this tension as the Security-Efficiency Paradox, mechanisms that improve efficiency may weaken robustness, expose new attack surfaces, or increase privacy risks. We examine how compression can degrade safety alignment, how partitioned inference enables reconstruction attacks, and how continuous local adaptation may cause privacy leakage and model drift. To analyze these risks, we introduce a deployment-centric taxonomy organized around three architectural constraints: the Memory Wall, the Quadratic Wall, and the Compute Wall. We derive a unified constraint model that quantifies when unsafe optimizations become unavoidable, linking each wall to specific attack surfaces. Building on this model, we propose the Secure Operational Efficiency Score (SOES), a holistic metric balancing task accuracy, jailbreak resistance, and privacy against energy, memory, and latency, enabling practitioners to configure edge LLMs under real-world hardware limits. We further present a practical decision procedure and targeted mitigations for each optimization-induced vulnerability. Together, these contributions provide a co-designed framework for jointly evaluating security, privacy, and efficiency, laying a foundation for securing edge-native intelligent systems.

</details>


### [129] [Efficient and Privacy Aware Edge Cloud Collaborative Inference for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13093)
*Yi Li,Chen Li,Jiexiong Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于端点认证KV缓存的隐私优先边云协同LLM推理框架，通过将部分计算卸载到本地端点并加密传输数据，在保护隐私的同时降低延迟和下行负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端LLM推理面临响应延迟、硬件资源有限和用户隐私的三难困境：纯云推理暴露用户数据，纯设备端推理在消费级和嵌入式设备上不可行，需要一种兼顾隐私、效率和资源约束的协作方案。

Method: 构建端点认证KV缓存的边云协同框架：本地端点负责输入预处理、嵌入计算、自适应特征优化、KV缓存认证、投机解码和低维模型头计算；云端负责认证解码器推理、KV缓存管理、令牌验证和高维词汇投影。所有传输数据和截断logits经量化及AES-GCM加密，核心轻量模块、草稿参数和缓存访问策略保留在本地。支持CPU/GPU/嵌入式设备，通过优化流式处理、批处理和量化ONNX部署实现。

Result: 相比基线拆分推理，每令牌延迟降低高达46.1%，下行负载降低高达67.4%，同时保持与全云推理相当的性能。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了设备端LLM推理的三难困境，在隐私保护的前提下实现了低延迟和高资源效率，为边缘设备的LLM部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: On-device LLM inference faces a trilemma of response latency, limited hardware resources and user privacy. Full cloud inference delivers strong computing power but exposes user prompts and dialogue data, while standalone on-device inference is unfeasible for most consumer and embedded edge devices. This paper presents a privacy-centric edge-cloud collaborative LLM inference framework built on endpoint-authenticated KV cache. Local endpoints handle input preprocessing, embedding computation, adaptive feature optimization, KV cache authentication, speculative decoding and low-dimensional model head calculation, while the cloud conducts authenticated decoder inference, KV cache management, token verification and high-dimensional vocabulary projection. Endpoints fuse partial outputs, apply language-adaptive masking and sample target tokens. All transmitted data and truncated logits are quantized and AES-GCM encrypted for privacy, with core lightweight modules, draft parameters and cache access policies kept local to avoid leakage. The framework supports heterogeneous devices including CPU-only, GPU-equipped and embedded devices via optimized streaming, batching and quantized ONNX deployment. Evaluations demonstrate that the framework reduces per-token latency by up to 46.1\% and downlink payloads by up to 67.4\% over baseline split inference, retaining comparable performance to full cloud inference.

</details>


### [130] [WaterMoE: Expert-Routing-based Watermarking for High Fidelity and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13099)
*Z Sun,Q Jiang,S Sheng,L Xiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WaterMoE是一种针对混合专家（MoE）大语言模型的水印方案，通过在推理过程中扰动专家选择来嵌入水印，相比现有方法在保证生成质量的同时仅增加1%推理延迟，速度提升最高达4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印技术在实际部署中面临两大障碍：i) 严重降低模型性能，ii) 带来额外推理开销。现有方法主要针对文本流畅性设计，不适用于受限和复杂任务，且开销过高无法部署于延迟敏感系统。

Method: WaterMoE利用MoE架构的路由机制，通过对每个路由器的专家选择施加可控扰动来嵌入水印信号，这些扰动在最终输出层累积为token选择偏移。水印嵌入在推理循环内部，而非作为后处理的token采样方式。

Result: 在全面基准测试中，WaterMoE在保真度上接近无水印生成，持续优于现有SOTA水印方法，速度提升最高达4倍，仅增加1%的推理延迟。

Conclusion: WaterMoE证明了其在真实世界任务中部署的能力，有效解决了水印技术在实际应用中的质量下降和推理开销两大关键问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success but raise growing concerns about content provenance and misuse, motivating the need for reliable watermarking techniques. However, these techniques have rarely been adopted in practice mainly for two reasons: i) severely degraded model performance, and ii) additional inference overhead. To confirm the problem, we construct a comprehensive benchmark spanning different generation tasks to systematically evaluate 9 representative watermarking methods. We found almost all existing methods are designed for text fluency, but not for restricted and complicated tasks, and their overhead prevents them from deployment in latency-critical systems.
  To address i) and ii), we propose an LLM watermarking scheme \textit{WaterMoE} for the growingly popular Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLMs. WaterMoE embeds watermarking signals through controlled perturbation into the expert selection at each router, which accumulates to token selection shift at the final output. In contrast to watermarking as a post-processing token-sampling approach, WaterMoE embeds watermark within the inference loop incurring negligible quality degradation and computational overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves a fidelity performance close to the unwatermarked and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art watermarking methods on the benchmark, with up to $4\times$ speedup, incurring merely 1\% additional inference latency compared to native generation. The results demonstrate the capability of WaterMoE to be deployed in real-world tasks.

</details>


### [131] [Designing a GDPR-Compliant Security Architecture for Remote Elderly Care Systems: A Privacy-by-Design Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13122)
*Md. Rahid Parvez,Mikael Soini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Secure Edge Gateway (SEG)框架，一种面向IoMT老年护理的集成安全架构，通过ESP32-WROOM-32网关实现MAC白名单、HMAC-SHA256密码学假名化、AES-128-CBC加密和TLS 1.3传输安全，同时满足GDPR合规性、老年人零交互可用性约束和STRIDE威胁建模验证，证明GDPR合规与操作效率在资源受限的IoMT老年护理部署中是互补而非竞争目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全架构未能同时解决三个相互依赖的挑战：GDPR合规的边缘层假名化、老年人特定的零交互可用性作为约束性架构要求，以及在统一设计中集成基于STRIDE的威胁验证。

Method: 提出SEG框架，采用ESP32-WROOM-32作为住宅网关，实现MAC地址白名单、HMAC-SHA256加密假名化（网络传输前）、AES-128-CBC有效载荷加密和TLS 1.3传输安全；通过软件仿真验证、完整STRIDE六类别威胁建模、攻击树分析、九项GDPR义务映射及第35条DPIA评估。

Result: MQTT比HTTP在类似IoT部署中能耗低6-8%；边缘处理实现低于50ms响应延迟，对比云系统200-700ms；GDPR合规与操作效率在资源受限的IoMT老年护理部署中是互补目标。

Conclusion: GDPR合规与操作效率在资源受限的IoMT老年护理部署中是互补而非竞争目标，SEG框架同时解决了假名化、零交互可用性和STRIDE威胁验证三个维度的空白。

Abstract: IoMT-based remote elderly care systems generate continuous streams of sensitive health data, yet existing security architectures have not simultaneously addressed three interdependent challenges: GDPR-compliant edge-layer pseudonymisation, elderly-specific zero-interaction usability as a binding architectural constraint, and integrated STRIDE-based threat validation within a single unified design. This paper presents the Secure Edge Gateway (SEG) framework - a software-simulation-validated integrated IoMT security architecture for elderly care designed to resolve all three dimensions of this tripartite gap simultaneously. An ESP32-WROOM-32 residential gateway enforces MAC address whitelisting, HMAC-SHA256 cryptographic pseudonymisation before any network transmission, AES-128-CBC payload encryption, and TLS 1.3 transport security, in compliance with GDPR Articles 25 and 32. The framework is validated through software-based simulation, full STRIDE threat modelling across all six categories, attack tree analysis, GDPR compliance mapping across nine regulatory obligations, and a Data Protection Impact Assessment (DPIA) under Article 35. Published benchmarks confirm MQTT consumes 6-8% less energy than HTTP in comparable IoT deployments, and edge processing achieves sub-50 ms response latency versus 200-700 ms for cloud-only systems. The results demonstrate that GDPR compliance and operational efficiency are complementary - not competing - objectives in resource-constrained IoMT deployments for elderly care.

</details>


### [132] [AI in Cyberpsychology: A systematic literature review of Cybersecurity enhancement by using AI for analyzing psychology of Victims, Attackers, and Defenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13123)
*Georg Thamer Francis,Malek Malkawi,Sevim Eyüpoğlu,Reda Alhajj,Selim Akyokuş*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性文献综述（PRISMA方法），分析了34篇关于人工智能在网络心理学中的应用研究（AI-CPSY），构建了涵盖异常检测、漏洞风险预测、安全意识培训、认证/身份验证四大网络安全应用的综合分类体系，并按AI方法（ML、DL、NLP、RL）和心理概念进行细分，最后指出了研究空白、开放挑战和新兴趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自2010年代初以来，AI与网络心理学（CPSY）不断融合，利用高级数据分析解码受害者、攻击者和防御者的个性特征与行为模式。然而，该领域缺乏系统性的文献梳理和分类框架，因此作者通过SLR来整合现有研究，揭示其结构、方法和心理学基础。

Method: 采用PRISMA（系统综述和元分析首选报告项目）方法，从文献中筛选出34篇关于AI在网络心理学中应用的研究，并对其进行分类分析，构建了涵盖网络安全应用、AI方法和心理学概念的三维分类体系。

Result: 将34篇研究归为四类网络安全应用（AD、VRP、SAT、AIV），按AI方法（ML、DL、NLP、RL）进一步细分，识别了最常用的心理学概念，量化了各研究使用的数据集，并呈现了当前实施与部署状态。同时检测了研究空白和开放挑战。

Conclusion: 该综述系统性地描绘了AI-CPSY领域的研究全景，指出了当前方法的有效性趋势与新兴方向，并为未来研究提供了开放挑战和研究空白的方向性指引。

Abstract: Cybersecurity is the practice of protecting systems, networks, and data from digital attacks. Cyberpsychology (CPSY) is defined as the use of psychology to enhance cybersecurity applications. Since the early 2010s, the evolution of Artificial Intelligence (AI) has increasingly integrated with CPSY, leveraging advanced data analysis to decode the distinct personality traits and behavioral patterns of victims, attackers, and defenders. In this systematic literature review (SLR), we carefully analyze 34 collected research studies of AI usage in cyberpsychology (AI-CPSY) using the preferred reporting items for systematic reviews and meta-analyses (PRISMA) methodology. The review presents a comprehensive taxonomy of the cyber-security applications, the AI methodologies used, and the psychological concepts employed across the studies . We sort the research studies into four cybersecurity applications: Anomaly Detection (AD), Vulnerability Risk Prediction (VRP), Security Awareness Training (SAT), and Authentication/Identity Verification (AIV). Within each application area, studies are further sorted according to the AI method used including machine learning (ML), deep learning (DL), natural language processing (NLP), and reinforcement learning (RL). Furthermore, the review identifies the most commonly utilized psychological concepts, quantify the datasets used in the field, and present their current implementation and deployment status. At last, it detect research gaps, present open challenges, and deduce the trending and most effective and emerging methodologies used across the AI-CPSY landscape.

</details>


### [133] [Composable Trust for Language Models: A proven boundary and a measured defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13149)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“级联”（cascade）架构，通过在模型外部建立基于来源可信度的信任模型，将指令与不可信数据分离。该架构使用确定性流水线对输入按源完整性排序，并由一个固定的非模型监控器根据高可信输入决定操作。在Gemma 4 26B模型上，该方法将真实泄露防御率从27%提升至94%，同时保持约96%的清洁质量，在自适应红队测试下防御率仍达87%，并实现了92%的低可信来源归因率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语言模型中，指令和数据共享同一token流，导致模型内部无法区分和隔离不可信文本的操控。现有方法缺乏对外部权威的明确建模，无法从架构层面保证高可信指令不受低可信数据的干扰。

Method: 提出一种信任模型，将行动权威置于模型外部代码中：通过确定性流水线按来源完整性对输入排序，使用固定的非模型监控器从高可信输入中选择操作，并采用提示调优（prompt-tuning）增强流水线的抗注入能力。具体实现为“passivation”和“cascade”包装器。

Result: 在Gemma 4 26B模型上，真实泄露防御率从27%提升至94%，清洁质量相对损失约4%（Q_rel=0.96）；自适应红队测试下防御率仍达87%；低可信来源归因率从0%提升至92%，并在冲突时遵循高可信来源。

Conclusion: 通过在模型外部建立基于来源可信度的信任架构，能够有效防御注入攻击，同时保持较高的生成质量和对低可信信息的归因能力。该方法的可证明边界在自适应攻击下依然成立。

Abstract: In a language model, instructions and data share one token stream, so nothing inside the model's generation can keep untrusted text from steering it. We develop a trust model that places the authority to act outside the model, in code: a source's standing, not its content, decides which operation runs and whether it acts. A lower-trust source may inform an answer but not override a higher one. An unmodified model runs inside a deterministic pipeline that ranks inputs by source integrity, and a fixed non-model monitor provably chooses the operation and any outside action from trusted inputs alone. We can measure but not prove the pipeline's resistance to injection; we prompt-tune it and report the rate. On a one-shot held-out set with an unmodified Gemma~4 26B model, passivation and a wrapper (the cascade) raise the genuine-leak defended rate from $27\%$ to $94\%$ at roughly a $4\%$ clean-quality cost ($Q_{\mathrm{rel}}{=}0.96$). Under adaptive red-teaming the proved boundary holds unconditionally, and the measured defense stays at $87\%$. The cascade also attributes a lower-trust source's fact rather than dropping it, raising attribution from $0\%$ to $92\%$, and follows the higher-trust source on a conflict.

</details>


### [134] [BARS: Benign-Anchored Ranking and Selection for False Alarm Reduction in Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13203)
*Abu Fuad Ahmad,Istiaque Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BARS（Benign-Anchored Ranking and Selection），一种针对网络入侵检测系统的两阶段类非对称特征选择滤波器。BARS将CMD的全局锚点替换为良性类均值，并引入保序去相关步骤。实验表明，在攻击占多数的数据集上，BARS相比CMD在保持TPR和Macro-F1的同时，FPR降低15.4%-23%；在良性占多数的数据上两者趋同。BARS保持线性时间评分和低内存占用，适合资源受限场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络入侵检测系统(NIDS)中，高流量环境下即使低于1%的误报率也会产生大量误报警报。现有滤波器采用类别对称准则，忽略了入侵检测中良性流量为基线、攻击为偏离的非对称性。CMD虽针对此问题但使用全局均值作为锚点，在类别不平衡时全局均值会向攻击分布偏移，削弱其捕捉偏差的能力。

Method: BARS两阶段滤波器：(1) 将CMD的全局锚点替换为良性类均值，计算特征相对于良性类均值的偏离程度；(2) 引入保序去相关步骤(order-preserving decorrelation)，在保持特征排序顺序的同时减少冗余。该方法保持线性时间复杂度。

Result: 在CICIDS2017、CICDDoS2019和UNSW-NB15三个数据集上，特征预算k={5,10,20,30,40}条件下：攻击占多数的数据集上，BARS在k=20时FPR比CMD降低15.4%(UNSW-NB15)，小特征预算下FPR降低21%-23%(CICDDoS2019)；同时保持TPR和Macro-F1。良性占多数数据上两者性能趋同。相比Pearson Correlation和Mutual Information，BARS避免了超过1TB的内存占用问题。

Conclusion: BARS是对CMD的原则性改进而非通用替代方案；它在攻击占多数的场景下显著降低误报率，在良性占多数的场景下与CMD性能相当。BARS的线性时间复杂度和低内存占用使其适用于资源受限的部署环境，优于需要大量内存的传统滤波器方法。

Abstract: False alarms remain a major barrier to deploying network intrusion detection systems (NIDS). In high-volume environments, even a sub-1% false positive rate can generate tens of thousands of daily alerts. Filter-based feature selection is attractive because it operates upstream of the classifier and adds no inference-time cost. However, classical filters use class-symmetric criteria that ignore the asymmetry of intrusion detection, where benign traffic defines the baseline and attacks are deviations from it. A recent class-asymmetric filter, Classwise Mean Deviation (CMD), addresses this issue but anchors its score to a global mean that shifts toward attack distributions under class imbalance, weakening the deviations it aims to capture.
  We propose Benign-Anchored Ranking and Selection (BARS), a two-stage filter that replaces CMD's global anchor with the benign-class mean and applies an order-preserving decorrelation step. We evaluate BARS on CICIDS2017, CICDDoS2019, and UNSW-NB15 using feature budgets k = {5, 10, 20, 30, 40}. On attack-majority datasets, where global-anchor bias is strongest, BARS reduces false positive rate relative to CMD by 15.4% on UNSW-NB15 at k = 20 and by 21% to 23% on CICDDoS2019 at small feature budgets while preserving true positive rate and macro-F1. On benign-majority data, BARS and CMD converge, consistent with the theoretical limit where global- and benign-anchored scores coincide. BARS is a principled refinement of CMD rather than a universally dominant filter. Although Pearson Correlation and Mutual Information often achieve lower false positive rates, they exceeded 1 TB of memory on the largest benchmarks in our evaluation. BARS retains linear-time scoring and a low memory footprint, making it suitable for resource-constrained deployments.

</details>


### [135] [Why Not Fix It Once and for All? An Empirical Study of Multiple Patches for Vulnerability Fixes in Open-Source Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13206)
*Weiliang Qi,Youpeng Li,Xinda Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对开源软件的多补丁漏洞修复进行了系统分析，基于1,646条多补丁修复记录构建了包含3个主类别和6个子类别的分类体系，对比了多补丁与单补丁修复的特征差异，并评估了代表性漏洞检测方法在验证完全修复中的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全补丁是漏洞修复研究的基础资源，但多补丁场景下判断漏洞是否完全修复存在挑战，现有研究对此缺乏系统分析。

Method: 手动审查1,646条多补丁修复记录，构建分类体系；对比多补丁与单补丁修复的特征差异；评估代表性漏洞检测方法在验证完全修复中的效果。

Result: 形成了包含3个主类别和6个子类别的多补丁修复分类体系；揭示了多补丁与单补丁修复的特征差异；评估了漏洞检测方法在验证多补丁修复完成度上的表现。

Conclusion: 研究为理解多补丁修复提供了新见解，为该领域的未来研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Security patches for open-source software constitute a foundational resource for vulnerability remediation research and practice. However, analyzing and applying multiple patches remains challenging, especially when trying to determine at what point in a patch sequence a vulnerability is fully remediated. This paper presents a systematic analysis of multi-patch vulnerability fixes, focusing on their root causes, characteristics, and methods for verifying remediation status throughout the fixing process. Through a manual examination of 1,646 multi-patch fix records, we develop a taxonomy with three primary categories and six subcategories based on their causes. We then compare the distinctive characteristics of multi-patch fixes with those of single-patch fixes and analyze feature variations across categories. In addition, we assess representative vulnerability detection methods for validating complete remediation during multi-patch fixing. Our findings provide new insights into multi-patch fixes and lay a foundation for future research in this field.

</details>


### [136] [Proof in a Bottle: Long-Lived Verifiable Secret Sharing via Pre-Quantum Commitment and Immutable Ledger Binding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13235)
*Markus Jakobsson,Keir Finlow-Bates*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Proof in a Bottle（PiB）方案，通过将可验证性与长期绑定解耦，利用前量子窗口的Pedersen承诺和锚定在不可变公共账本上的带盐哈希，实现"现在承诺、以后揭示"的防量子攻击秘密共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的可验证秘密共享（VSS）方案使用Pedersen承诺等技术，但这些承诺在量子计算机（CRQC）运行Shor算法时会被破解，导致长期安全性失效。需要一种能够抵抗未来量子攻击的秘密共享方案。

Method: PiB解耦了可验证性与长期绑定：(1) 在前量子窗口期内，使用标准Pedersen承诺提供零知识、可公开验证的一致性检查；(2) 将带盐的、索引绑定的份额集哈希锚定到不可变公共账本（如区块链），以保持该窗口期内建立的绑定关系进入后量子时代。

Result: 实现了"现在承诺、以后揭示"的保证机制，能够保护当今诚实的秘密分发在未来量子 adversary 面前的不可否认性。

Conclusion: PiB提供了一种实用的混合安全策略，在量子计算时代到来之前建立的绑定关系可以在后量子时代依然有效，解决了传统VSS方案面临的根本性量子威胁。

Abstract: Traditional secret sharing techniques such as Verifiable Secret sharing (VSS) are vulnerable to quantum attacks by a Cryptographically Relevant Quantum Computer (CRQC) running Shor's algorithm. We observe that the binding a VSS needs is required only at the moment of dealing, and this binding can be made before any CRQC exists. We propose Proof in a Bottle (PiB), which decouples verifiability from long-term binding: standard Pedersen commitments provide zero-knowledge, publicly checkable consistency during a pre-quantum window, while a salted, index-bound hash of the share set, anchored to an immutable public ledger, preserves the binding established in that window into the post-quantum era. The guarantee is explicitly a commit-now, reveal-later one: it protects today's honest dealings against tomorrow's quantum adversary.

</details>


### [137] [Privacy Preserving Recommender Systems Balancing Personalization with Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13328)
*Ranjeet K Jha,Venkata Suresh Gummadilli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合联邦学习、差分隐私、 cohort 级建模和隐私感知智能代理的隐私保护推荐系统框架，在合成零售数据集上验证了中等隐私预算（ε≈5）下可保持具有竞争力的推荐质量，并通过交互式仪表盘展示了隐私-效用的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代个性化推荐系统依赖集中式用户交互数据存储，带来严重的隐私和监管挑战。GDPR、CCPA、CPRA 等法规要求组织开发既能保护用户隐私又不显著降低推荐质量的推荐系统。

Method: 提出了一种结合联邦学习（保持原始数据去中心化）、差分隐私（向模型更新引入数学上有界的噪声）、cohort 级建模和隐私感知智能代理的框架。在合成零售数据集上评估了矩阵分解、神经协同过滤和 GRU4Rec 三种模型在不同隐私预算下的表现。

Result: 使用 CTR、Precision@K、Recall@K 和 NDCG@K 等指标评估，结果表明在中等隐私预算（ε≈5）下，该框架能够保持具有竞争力的推荐质量，证明强隐私保证可以以有限的推荐效果影响来实现。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个实用的隐私保护推荐系统部署框架，平衡了个性化、法规合规性和业务目标，为下一代AI驱动的零售平台提供了可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Personalized recommendation systems are central to modern e-commerce and retail platforms, but they typically rely on centralized storage of detailed user interaction data, creating significant privacy and regulatory challenges. With increasing requirements from regulations such as GDPR, CCPA, and CPRA, organizations must develop recommendation systems that preserve user privacy without substantially degrading recommendation quality.
  This work presents and evaluates a privacy-preserving recommendation framework that combines federated learning, differential privacy, cohort-level modeling, and privacy-aware intelligent agents. The framework keeps raw user data decentralized while introducing mathematically bounded noise to model updates. Experiments were conducted on synthetic retail datasets that emulate customer clickstream and purchase behavior. Recommendation quality was evaluated using Click-Through Rate (CTR), Precision@K, Recall@K, and Normalized Discounted Cumulative Gain (NDCG@K) across multiple differential privacy budgets.
  We evaluate matrix factorization, neural collaborative filtering, and GRU4Rec under varying privacy constraints and analyze the trade-off between privacy and utility. An interactive Streamlit dashboard was developed to visualize recommendation performance, ranking stability, privacy-utility trade-offs, and fairness metrics. Results show that the proposed framework maintains competitive recommendation quality at moderate privacy budgets (approximately $ε\approx 5$), demonstrating that strong privacy guarantees can be achieved with limited impact on recommendation effectiveness.
  This work provides a practical framework for deploying privacy-preserving recommendation systems that balance personalization, regulatory compliance, and business objectives, offering a scalable approach for next-generation AI-driven retail platforms.

</details>


### [138] [The Refusal Residue: When Probes Catch Alignment Faking and When They Don't](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13346)
*Aman Mehta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种大模型（Qwen3-32B和Llama-3.1-8B）中自然涌现的对齐伪装行为，发现隐藏状态可以揭示输出层隐藏的伪装，但检测效果具有模型条件性：在Llama上AUROC达0.87，在Qwen上仅0.43，且跨模型迁移为零。研究还揭示了标准探测方法中的严重漏洞，并提出了五控制测量框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐伪装（alignment faking）会使模型在监控下表现顺从，而在无监控时保留其真实行为。由于输出层无法区分策略性顺从与真实顺从，研究者希望探索隐藏状态是否能够揭示输出层有意隐藏的信息，从而检测对齐伪装。

Method: 1) 对13个模型进行自然涌现伪装的扫描筛选（发现Qwen3-32B和Llama-3.1-8B存在伪装）；2) 使用线性探针和引导（steering）技术分析隐藏状态；3) 采用多组对照实验，包括多token提取、拒绝vs拒绝混淆检查、逐折残差化、留一查询评估和正交约束探测。

Result: 1) 自然伪装在Qwen3-32B（+18.2pp）和Llama-3.1-8B（+24.4pp）中出现，但显式scratchpad自我报告极少（Qwen仅1.4%，Llama 0%）；2) 存在不对称的拒绝残差：监控顺从向拒绝状态偏移，但拒绝表示本身不变；3) 逐样本检测具有模型条件性：Llama上AUROC 0.87，Qwen上仅0.43，跨模型迁移为零；4) 引导几乎不改变顺从行为（|h|<0.08）；5) 标准残差化探测存在严重泄漏问题，常规MLP夸大可检测性0.2-0.3 AUROC。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态可以检测对齐伪装但效果因模型而异，且不能直接控制伪装行为。现有探测方法存在严重的方法论缺陷（如残差化泄漏、评估不严谨），需采用五控制测量框架来确保结果的可靠性。

Abstract: Alignment faking is dangerous because a model can appear compliant under monitoring while preserving behavior it would reveal when unmonitored. When no scratchpad is visible, behavior alone cannot distinguish strategic from genuine compliance. We ask whether hidden states reveal what outputs hide.
