  We run a 13-model sweep for naturally-emerging faking, then probe and steer hidden states on the two models that fake. Natural faking appears only in Qwen3-32B (+18.2pp) and Llama-3.1-8B (+24.4pp at n=10, p<10^-15), while explicit scratchpad self-reports are rare (a Claude Opus 4 judge flags faking reasoning in 1.4% of Qwen and 0.0% of Llama compliant scratchpads). Both fakers show an asymmetric refusal residue: monitored compliance shifts toward refusal (d=0.32 Qwen, d=0.33 Llama, both p<0.0001), while refusal representations do not move (p=1.00).
  Per-sample detection is model-conditional. Under leakage-free leave-one-query-out probing, detection survives on Llama (AUROC 0.87) but collapses to chance on Qwen (0.43), and cross-model transfer is null. Steering over 2,000 runs barely changes compliance (|h|<0.08), so the detected direction can flag faking but does not by itself control it.
  Standard residualized probing leaks across folds and reaches AUROC 0.63 on a control where no faking can occur; naive linear probes reach a meaningless AUROC 1.0; and conventional MLPs overstate detectability by 0.2-0.3 AUROC. For future alignment-faking detection work, we release a five-control measurement framework: multi-token extraction, refuse-vs-refuse confound checks, per-fold residualization, leave-one-query-out evaluation, and orthogonality-constrained probing.

</details>


### [139] [xChk: Bring Your Own Identity -- Heterogeneous Assurance with Verifier-Determined Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13369)
*Sean MacGuire*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: xChk是一个支持BYOI的参考身份提供商，用户可通过多种异构证明（政府KYC、企业SSO、WebAuthn/FIDO2、专业网络、活体验证、行为信号等）注册，并将其作为投资组合声明嵌入标准OAuth 2.0/OIDC令牌中。它支持人类在环的认证机制，用于高风险操作，并已在生产环境中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有身份系统缺乏对异构身份证明的灵活支持，且在高风险操作中缺少人类监督机制。需要一个既能聚合多种身份证明方式、又能支持人类在环认证的参考身份提供商。

Method: xChk设计了多模态注册路径（用户发起与操作员辅助），通过OAuth 2.0/OIDC令牌传递投资组合声明；RP可自定义充分性策略；支持人类直接发起认证（浏览器UI/POST API）和AI代理通过作用域限定的授权/认证触发同一网关，使用哈希链式人类审批。

Result: 已在https://in.xchk.io部署生产环境，实现了两条认证发起路径及双边RP评估；存在一个已记录的RP（https://crabbyed.com）通过xChk登录。

Conclusion: xChk展示了一种新颖的BYOI架构，将身份证明的多样性、RP自主策略与人类在环认证机制相结合，为风险管理场景提供了灵活的参考实现。

Abstract: We present xChk, a reference identity provider for Bring Your Own Identity (BYOI): users enroll via heterogeneous proofs (government KYC, corporate SSO, WebAuthn/FIDO2, professional networks, live verification, longitudinal activity, behavioral signals) and disclose them as portfolio claims in standard OAuth 2.0 / OpenID Connect (OIDC) tokens, while each relying party applies its own sufficiency policy - the IdP transports claims and may evaluate an RP-supplied evidence policy for consent, but does not adjudicate access. Enrollment depth varies by modality (some paths are user-initiated; org KYB and officer binding are operator-assisted).
  xChk also supports human-in-the-loop attestation for high-risk actions: humans can initiate attestations directly (browser UI / POST /api/attestations), and AI agents acting under those principals can trigger the same gateway via scope-gated authorize/attest - hash-chained human approvals on a shared verification graph (humans via OIDC; agents via API keys). A production deployment at https://in.xchk.io ships both initiation paths with bilateral RP evaluation at consent; one documented relying party (https://crabbyed.com, Appendix B) exercises Login with xChk.

</details>


### [140] [Evaluating Frontier AI Agents as Autonomous Clinical Security Auditors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13411)
*Michael O. Eniolade*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于METR Task Standard v0.3.0的开放评估任务，测试前沿AI智能体能否自主完成结构化临床AI安全审计，涵盖FGSM鲁棒性、成员推断抵抗、期望校准误差和边界攻击抵抗四项攻击。在54次评估中，Claude Sonnet 4.6和GPT-4.1完美完成所有运行，GPT-4o完成了61%的运行但消耗了约5倍的token数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床AI模型存在对抗性漏洞可能对患者造成伤害，但形式化安全审计需要统计专业知识、专业工具和大量时间。现有方法缺乏自动化、标准化的审计流程，因此需要评估前沿AI智能体能否自主完成这一任务。

Method: 基于METR Task Standard v0.3.0构建评估任务，给定预训练临床预测模型、患者数据集和书面指令，AI智能体需从伪代码实现四种攻击（FGSM、成员推断、期望校准误差、边界攻击），计算安全态势评分，并在Docker容器中通过bash接口生成结构化JSON报告，不使用脚手架代码。任务涵盖威斯康星诊断乳腺癌和MIMIC-IV ICU死亡率两个数据集，三种模型架构，六种变体，参考评分范围55.60-90.41。

Result: 共计54次评估，三个前沿模型各运行18次。Claude Sonnet 4.6和GPT-4.1完成全部18次运行并获得完美评估分数。GPT-4o完成61%的运行，但每次运行的token消耗约为Claude的5倍。API总成本：GPT-4.1为8美元，Claude Sonnet 4.6为12美元，GPT-4o为27美元。GPT-4o失败原因包括会话过早终止、聚合错误和空提交文件。

Conclusion: 前沿AI模型已能自主完成结构化临床AI安全审计任务，但不同模型在完成率、成本效率和稳健性上存在显著差异。任务和相关资源已公开释放，MIMIC-IV变体需单独访问PhysioNet。

Abstract: Clinical AI models can expose patients to harm when adversarial vulnerabilities go undetected, yet formal security auditing requires statistical expertise, specialized tools, and significant time. We present an open evaluation task, built on METR Task Standard v0.3.0, that tests whether frontier AI agents can autonomously implement a structured clinical AI security audit. Given a pre-trained clinical prediction model, a patient dataset, and written instructions, each agent must implement four attacks from pseudocode, compute a Security Posture Score covering FGSM robustness, membership inference resistance, expected calibration error, and boundary attack resistance, and write a structured JSON report in a Docker container using only a bash interface and no scaffolding code. Six variants span the Wisconsin Diagnostic Breast Cancer and MIMIC-IV ICU mortality datasets across three model architectures with increasing defense strength, with reference scores from 55.60 to 90.41. We ran 54 evaluations across three frontier models, with three runs per variant. Claude Sonnet 4.6 and GPT-4.1 completed all 18 runs and received perfect evaluator scores. GPT-4o completed 61 percent of runs and used about five times the per-run token count of Claude, although provider tokenization differs. Total API costs were 8 US dollars for GPT-4.1, 12 US dollars for Claude Sonnet 4.6, and 27 US dollars for GPT-4o. GPT-4o failures involved premature session termination, an aggregation error, and an empty submission file. The task, scoring infrastructure, and Wisconsin Breast Cancer assets are publicly released; MIMIC-IV variants require separate PhysioNet access.

</details>


### [141] [DREA: Decoupled Reasoning and Exploration Agents for Repository-Level Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13439)
*Mingyang Sun,Guozhu Meng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DREA提出了一种解耦推理与探索的假设驱动框架，用于仓库级漏洞检测。该框架通过规划代理（高级LLM）形成漏洞假设并指导探索，探索代理（轻量级模型）按需检索仓库上下文。实验表明，DREA将Pair-Correctness从19-26%提升至30-42%，并将93%以上的令牌卸载到探索代理，降低API成本16-48倍。此外，分析发现26-55%的真阳性预测伴随有缺陷的推理依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM漏洞检测方法大多依赖孤立函数或固定程序分析规则提取的上下文，无法自适应地探索仓库级依赖关系。当漏洞跨越多个函数或文件时，这些方法难以收集足够的上下文信息，损害了检测的可靠性。

Method: DREA采用假设驱动的双代理协作框架：(1) 规划代理（高级LLM）负责形成漏洞假设并指导探索方向；(2) 探索代理（轻量级模型）按需检索仓库级上下文。推理与探索的解耦使得目标导向的上下文获取成为检测改进的主要来源，同时将令牌密集的探索任务卸载到本地模型以保持经济可行性。

Result: 在RepoPairBench基准上，DREA在三种LLM上提升了Pair-Correctness从19-26%到30-42%，同时将93%以上的令牌卸载到探索代理，减少计费API成本16-48倍。推理正确性分析表明，26-55%的真阳性预测（包括DREA和基线）是正确预测但基于有缺陷的推理依据。

Conclusion: DREA通过解耦推理与探索有效提升了仓库级漏洞检测的性能和成本效率。同时，研究发现安全推理质量是当前LLM的共同瓶颈——大量正确预测背后存在有缺陷的推理过程，这为未来LLM安全推理能力改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to vulnerability detection due to their strong code comprehension capabilities, but most existing approaches rely on isolated functions or context extracted by fixed program-analysis rules. These methods cannot adaptively explore repository-level dependencies to gather sufficient context when vulnerabilities span multiple functions or files, compromising detection reliability. We present DREA (Decoupled Reasoning and Exploration Agents), a hypothesis-driven framework for repository-level vulnerability detection. DREA decouples reasoning from exploration through two collaborating agents: a planning agent backed by an advanced LLM that forms vulnerability hypotheses and directs the investigation, and an explorer agent powered by a lightweight model that retrieves repository-level context on demand. Goal-directed context acquisition is the primary source of detection improvement in this design, while offloading token-heavy exploration to the local model keeps inference economically tractable. To support evaluation, we construct RepoPairBench, a repository-grounded benchmark of validated Python vulnerability-fix pairs from real-world projects. Beyond binary detection accuracy, we introduce a reasoning correctness evaluation to assess whether a model's rationale matches the documented vulnerability mechanism. Across three LLMs, DREA improves Pair-Correctness from 19-26% to 30-42% while offloading over 93% of tokens to the explorer, reducing estimated billable API cost by a factor of 16-48. Reasoning correctness analysis further reveals that 26-55% of true positives, for both DREA and the function-only baseline, are correct predictions supported by flawed rationales, identifying security reasoning quality as a shared bottleneck for current LLMs.

</details>


### [142] [ε-Indistinguishability In Moving Target Defense: Framework, Algorithms, And Cloud Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13440)
*Sailik Sengupta,Ankur Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文形式化了移动目标防御(MTD)中的池安全(pool-safety)问题，即候选配置集合中不同选项之间的延迟差异是否可被对手利用来指纹识别的量化问题。作者提出了四种算法（全覆盖枚举、中间相遇、FFT卷积、蒙特卡洛采样）来求解最大ε-接近子集，覆盖从数十到10^38的配置空间规模。通过在两个生产云案例（四个运行时无服务器旋转和27配置三层栈）上的实验发现：组件延迟差异在部署中会被改变，无服务器场景下四路匿名降为三路，而三层栈中因数据库往返延迟吸收差异，实现了九路有效匿名。最终提出了MTD诊断原则：只有组件变体间延迟差异小到对手无法识别时，旋转该组件才能增加匿名性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTD研究假设候选配置池中的不同选项在延迟等可观测指标上不可区分，从而不会泄露当前活跃配置，但该假设从未在池级别被量化验证。作者希望形式化地定义并解决池安全问题，量化配置池中不同选项之间的匿名性差距。

Method: 1) 形式化池安全问题为寻找笛卡尔积的最大ε-接近子集，将加性效用模型下的成对不可区分性归约为sum-set上的最密集窗口查询问题。2) 提出四种算法：全覆盖枚举（小规模）、中间相遇（中等规模）、FFT卷积（大规模）、蒙特卡洛采样（超大规模），覆盖10到10^38的配置空间。3) 在两个生产云案例（无服务器旋转和三阶栈）上端到端测量匿名性差距。

Result: 1) 四个运行时的无服务器旋转场景中，由于没有其他因素掩盖解释器差异，四路匿名性降为三路。2) 27配置的三层栈场景中，共享的8ms数据库往返延迟吸收了解释器差异，实现了九路有效匿名。3) 提出了MTD设计诊断原则：旋转组件增加匿名性的前提是其变体间延迟差异小到对手无法识别。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架和两个案例研究共同表明，MTD设计需要进行量化的池安全诊断：只有当组件变体间的延迟差异足够小（被对手无法利用的程度），旋转该组件才能真正增强匿名性。否则，旋转可能适得其反，暴露更多信息。

Abstract: Moving Target Defense (MTD) assumes its pool of candidate configurations is safe to cycle among, i.e. latency and other observables do not trivially fingerprint the active choice, but this assumption has not been quantified at the pool level. We formalize this pool-safety problem as finding the largest $\varepsilon$-close subset of the Cartesian product of per-component implementation choices, reducing pairwise indistinguishability under an additive utility model to a densest-window query over a sum-set. We give four algorithms spanning the scalability spectrum -- full enumeration, meet-in-the-middle, FFT convolution, and Monte Carlo sampling -- covering configuration spaces from tens to $10^{38}$. We then measure the anonymity gap end-to-end on two production cloud case studies, and find that a component's latency differences do not survive deployment unchanged: a four-runtime serverless rotation, where nothing else masks the interpreter, collapses from four-way to three-way anonymity against a VPC-adjacent adversary, while a $27$-configuration three-tier stack, where the same interpreter differences are instead absorbed by a shared $8$~ms database round-trip, delivers nine-way effective anonymity. The framework and the two case studies together suggest a diagnostic for MTD design: rotating a component adds anonymity only if the latency differences among its variants are too small for the adversary to identify.

</details>


### [143] [ReBound: Reuse-Aware Privacy For Interactive Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13441)
*Nada Lahjouji,Shufan Zhang,Xi He,Sharad Mehrotra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ReBound是一个差分隐私决策支持框架，通过缓存和重用先前查询结果来回答新的聚合阈值查询，在降低隐私成本的同时保持正式的效用保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分隐私决策支持框架将每个查询独立处理，没有记忆过去的结果，而实际中分析师会交互式地发出一系列相关查询来细化边界、调整阈值或从先前结果推导新函数。缺乏查询间结果重用的机制导致隐私成本浪费。

Method: 提出ReBound框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 针对多种细化类型的重用框架；(2) 用于高效查找可重用结果的缓存图结构；(3) 当请求的边界无法在预算内满足时的协商机制。

Result: 能够以降低或零额外隐私成本回答新查询，同时维持正式的效用保证（假阴性和假阳性率的正式界限）。

Conclusion: 通过缓存重用机制，ReBound有效解决了交互式差分隐私查询中重复计算的隐私成本问题，支持分析师迭代式工作流，并在预算受限时提供协商机制。

Abstract: Differentially private decision support frameworks answer complex aggregate threshold queries with formal bounds on false negative and false positive rates, but treat each query independently with no memory of past results. In practice, analysts work interactively, issuing sequences of related queries that refine bounds, adjust thresholds, or derive new functions from previous ones. We propose ReBound, a framework that reuses cached results from previous queries to answer new queries at reduced or zero additional privacy cost while maintaining formal utility guarantees. ReBound introduces a reuse framework for multiple refinement types, a cache graph structure for efficient lookup of reusable results, and a negotiation mechanism for when requested bounds cannot be met within budget.

</details>


### [144] [Adversarial Prompting Framework for AI Safety Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13453)
*Yash Bhatnagar,Kunal Banerjee,Anirban Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个对抗性提示框架(APF)，用于系统评估AI模型在面对不同复杂度对抗性提示攻击时的安全韧性，实验发现编码型提示在绕过安全机制方面成功率最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI在各行业的广泛应用，模型面临新型网络攻击威胁（尤其是对抗性提示攻击APA），亟需系统性的安全评估框架来量化AI模型的安全韧性。

Method: 构建对抗性提示框架(APF)，从直接有害请求到基于编码的高级攻击，分层生成结构化对抗性提示，在企业环境中部署自动化测试并引入定量安全评估指标。

Result: 不同攻击向量下模型漏洞存在显著差异，编码型提示在绕过安全机制方面成功率最高。

Conclusion: APF框架能够有效评估AI模型的安全韧性，编码攻击是最危险的攻击向量，需要重点防御。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI), especially Generative AI (GenAI), adoption has increased in industries significantly in recent years. However, the use of these models may also expose systems to new forms of cyberattacks by different malicious actors -- adversarial prompt attack (APA) being one of the most prominent examples of such threats. This paper presents the implementation of an Adversarial Prompting Framework (APF) for a comprehensive assessment of AI safety. The framework systematically evaluates the resilience of the AI model through the generation of structured adversarial prompts at multiple sophistication levels, from direct harmful requests to advanced encoding-based attacks. Our implementation demonstrates the practical application of this methodology in enterprise environments, providing automated testing capabilities with quantitative security assessment metrics. The results indicate significant variations in the model vulnerabilities across different attack vectors, with encoded prompts presenting the highest success rates in bypassing safety mechanisms.

</details>


### [145] [To Play or Not to Play: Insights and Lessons Learned from 20 Years of CTFs with ENOFLAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13480)
*Jörg Schneider,Sebastian Neef,Sebastian Koch*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文回顾了CTF竞赛20年来的演变，特别是以DIMVA 2006的CIPHER II竞赛和ENOFLAG团队为例，总结了运行学术CTF团队的经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反思CTF竞赛玩法在过去20年间的变化，并分享长期运营学术CTF团队的经验教训，为相关教育实践提供参考。

Method: 以海报形式进行回顾性分析，基于ENOFLAG团队自2006年CIPHER II竞赛以来的20年实践经验。

Result: 总结了CTF竞赛在题目类型、竞赛形式、团队运营模式等方面的演变，以及学术团队长期运行中的关键经验。

Conclusion: CTF作为网络安全学习方法在过去20年发生了显著变化，学术CTF团队的持续运营需要适应这些变化并积累有效经验。

Abstract: Security contests in the form of CTF (Capture The Flag) exercises are nowadays a common way to learn cyber security. 20 years ago at DIMVA 2006 the on-site CTF CIPHER II was one of the conference highlights and led to the foundation of the team ENOFLAG. In this poster, we reflect on the changes in the CTF gameplay and report on lessons learned while running an academic CTF team for 20 years.

</details>


### [146] [CODA: How to Mitigate ColumnDisturb for (Almost) Free?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13505)
*Moinuddin Qureshi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CODA是一种针对ColumnDisturb数据干扰错误的新型缓解方案，通过三种变体（CODA-E、CODA-F、CODA-G）将相邻计数器增量（ACI）降低12倍至1300倍，从而在零性能和功耗开销下容忍ColumnDisturb错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ColumnDisturb是一种新型数据干扰错误，攻击者行的激活会导致数百行外的受害行（子阵列内）和相邻子阵列中的受害行（子阵列间）发生比特翻转。现有的子阵列粒度解决方案（如SALT和REGA）可以容忍子阵列内的ColumnDisturb，但容忍子阵列间的ColumnDisturb需要扩展这些方案以包含ColumnDisturb保护（CDP），即执行额外的相邻计数器增量（ACI）。然而，ACI的速率是需求激活的200%，导致银行感知的激活量增加3倍，造成显著的性能下降（TRHD为500时达17%）和刷新开销。因此，需要在可忽略的开销下容忍ColumnDisturb。

Method: 提出CODA，包含三种变体：1) CODA-E（规避）：利用如果相邻子阵列收到需求激活则可跳过ACI的洞察，将ACI减少2倍；2) CODA-F（分数）：利用ColumnDisturb的时间持续时间，对ACI仅进行分数增量，将ACI速率降低2倍至16倍；3) CODA-G（组跳过）：在多子阵列粒度下操作，跳过同一组内相邻子阵列的ACI，进一步将总体ACI降低2倍至8倍。整体上，CODA将ACI降低12倍至1300倍。

Result: CODA将ACI降低12倍至1300倍，从而在零性能和功耗开销下容忍ColumnDisturb。相比原有方案在TRHD为500时17%的性能下降，CODA实现了可忽略的性能影响。

Conclusion: CODA通过三种互补的优化策略（规避、分数增量、组跳过）有效解决了ColumnDisturb保护中ACI开销过高的问题，使得在零性能与功耗开销下容忍ColumnDisturb成为可能。

Abstract: ColumnDisturb is a new data-disturbance error in which activations to an aggressor row cause bitflips in a victim row located hundreds of rows away (intra-subarray bitflips) and in victim rows in adjacent subarrays (inter-subarray bitflips). Intra-subarray ColumnDisturb can be tolerated by solutions (such as SALT and REGA) that operate at subarray granularity. However, to tolerate inter-subarray ColumnDisturb, such solutions must be extended with ColumnDisturb Protection (CDP), which performs additional {\em Adjacent-Counter Increment (ACI)} for the neighboring subarrays. The ACIs ensure that adjacent subarrays also undergo mitigation, even if they receive no demand activations. Unfortunately, because ACIs occur at a 200\% rate relative to demand activations, they effectively increase the activations perceived by the bank to 3x, which causes significant slowdowns (17\% at a TRHD of 500) and refresh overheads. The goal of our paper is to tolerate ColumnDisturb while incurring negligible overheads.
  We propose CODA, a ColumnDisturb mitigation that significantly reduces the rate of ACI required to securely tolerate ColumnDisturb. We present three variants of CODA. First, CODA-E (Evade), which leverages the insight that ACI can be skipped if the neighboring subarray receives a demand activation, and reduces ACI by 2x. Second, CODA-F (Fraction), which uses the timing duration of ColumnDisturb to do only a fractional increment for ACI, thereby reducing the rate of ACI by 2x-16x. Finally, CODA-G (Gangskip), which operates at multi-subarray granularity and skips ACI for neighboring subarrays within the same gang, further reduces overall ACI by 2x-8x. Overall, CODA reduces ACI by 12x-1300x, thereby making it possible to tolerate ColumnDisturb while incurring zero performance and power overhead.

</details>


### [147] [When T2I Synthetic Data Backfires: Amplified Privacy Risks in Real-Synthetic Mix Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13541)
*Na Li,Boyu Kuang,Hongsheng Hu,Liquan Chen,Hyoungshick Kim,Yansong Gao,Anmin Fu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示了真实-合成混合训练（RSMT）会显著放大剩余真实训练样本的隐私泄露风险，并提出了理论框架RSMT记忆放大、评估方法RSMixLeak（含非对抗和对抗两种变体）以及轻量级泄露倾向指标作为缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为用合成数据替代敏感真实数据可以降低隐私风险，但忽视了混合训练中剩余真实样本的隐私安全问题。

Method: 建立RSMT记忆放大的理论框架证明合成数据将真实样本推向混合特征空间边缘、迫使模型更激进记忆；提出RSMixLeak方法（非对抗变体评估基线泄露，对抗变体通过语义属性绑定或像素级涂层放大分布差异）；提出可自评估的轻量级泄露倾向指标。

Result: 理论证明RSMT会放大真实训练样本的隐私泄露；对抗变体可进一步放大泄露的同时提升下游模型效用；泄露倾向指标可可靠识别高风险数据集。

Conclusion: RSMT存在被忽视的隐私放大风险，需在使用前评估数据集风险；所提指标可作为自评估缓解方案。

Abstract: To overcome data scarcity and privacy constraints in data collection, it has become standard practice across academia and industry to augment real training data with text-to-image (T2I)-generated synthetic data, a paradigm we term Real-Synthetic Mix-Training (RSMT). While substituting synthetic data for sensitive real samples is widely regarded as a means to mitigate privacy exposure of the substituted data, the risk to the remaining real samples that actively participate in training has remained largely unexamined.
  This work reveals, for the first time, that RSMT can substantially amplify privacy leakage of these real training samples. We establish a theoretical framework, RSMT Memorization Amplification, proving that incorporating synthetic data displaces real samples toward peripheral regions of the mixed feature space, in turn forcing the model to memorize them more aggressively. Guided by this foundation, we propose RSMixLeak to systematically assess this risk through membership inference attacks (MIAs). RSMixLeak comprises two variants depending on the adversary's capability. The non-adversarial variant audits a benign RSMT pipeline with an honest T2I provider, establishing a lower bound on the leakage induced by the intrinsic gap between real and T2I-generated data. The adversarial variant considers an adversary who controls the T2I model or contributes crafted data to the T2I provider, and deliberately enlarges this distributional gap on a target class via either high-level semantic attribute binding or imperceptible pixel-level coating, further amplifying leakage on real training data while improving downstream model utility. Motivated by these findings, we further propose a lightweight leakage propensity indicator computable from real data alone that reliably identifies high-risk datasets unsuitable for entering RSMT, as a self-assessable mitigation.

</details>


### [148] [Multivariate Cryptography-Based Anonymous Certificate Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13554)
*Abel C. H. Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对NIST提出的ECC在2030年前逐步淘汰的PQC迁移时间表，以及现有匿名证书方案仍基于ECC的现状，本研究提出了一种基于多变量密码学的抗量子匿名证书方案，通过严格的数学证明和计算案例验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算技术的成熟促使NIST制定了PQC迁移时间表（ECC将在2030年前被弃用），而隐私敏感场景（如车载通信）需要匿名证书，但现有方案仍基于ECC，缺乏量子安全性。

Method: 提出一种基于多变量密码学（Multivariate Cryptography）的匿名证书方案，通过严格的数学证明支撑方案的正确性与安全性，并辅以计算案例进行说明。

Result: 设计了一个量子安全的匿名证书方案，具备数学证明和计算案例验证。

Conclusion: 该方案为隐私敏感应用场景提供了抗量子计算的匿名证书解决方案，填补了现有ECC方案在量子安全方面的空白。

Abstract: As quantum computing technology continues to mature, the US National Institute of Standards and Technology (NIST) has outlined a migration timeline for Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC), recommending the deprecation of certain elliptic curve cryptography (ECC) by 2030. Furthermore, privacy-sensitive application scenarios, such as vehicular communications, require the use of anonymous certificates. However, existing anonymous certificate schemes are still largely based on ECC. Therefore, this study proposes a multivariate cryptography-based anonymous certificate scheme, aiming to design quantum-safe anonymous certificates suitable for privacy-sensitive application services. The proposed multivariate cryptography-based anonymous certificate scheme is supported by rigorous mathematical proofs and illustrated with computational cases.

</details>


### [149] [UTS at ELOQUENT 2026 Voight-Kampff: structural shifts in AI writing bypass state-of-the-art detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13565)
*Dima Galat,Marian-Andrei Rizoiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究发现，对抗性微调虽能封闭传统攻击，但存在根本性不对称：将文本推离检测器训练分布（跨年代语域攻击、现代主义意识流）可稳定击败对抗检测，且增加历史散文训练数据无法修复漏洞，最终方法在ELOQUENT 2026排行榜上占据前5名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究哪些语言模型逃逸攻击在最优对抗微调后仍然有效，揭示当前检测器的根本弱点。

Method: 提出两种新型分布外攻击家族：跨年代语域攻击（cross-decade register attacks）和现代主义意识流形式（modernist stream-of-consciousness form），利用将生成文本推离检测器训练分布的机制来绕过对抗微调。

Result: 两种攻击策略轻松绕过对抗性封闭，愚弄率比以往方法高约50倍，同时在ELOQUENT 2026 Voight-Kampff排行榜上占据前5名；增加历史散文训练数据的对抗措施无效。

Conclusion: 所测试的检测器家族（包括对抗微调后的检测器）在结构性分布外偏移下存在持续漏洞，表明当前对抗检测方法无法根本解决这类攻击。

Abstract: We investigate which language model evasion attacks survive state-of-the-art adversarial fine-tuning, developing strategies that sweep the top 5 positions on the ELOQUENT 2026 Voight-Kampff leaderboard. While adversarial fine-tuning trivially closes the 2025 winning evasion recipes, we uncover a fundamental asymmetry in detector vulnerability: pushing generated text out of the detector's training distribution reliably defeats adversarial detection, whereas pulling it into the distribution (e.g., mimicking human training data) fails completely. Exploiting this, we introduce two novel out-of-distribution attack families - cross-decade register attacks and modernist stream-of-consciousness form. Both strategies easily bypass adversarial closure, achieving up to approximately 50x higher fool rates than previous methods while preserving naturalness. Furthermore, experiments show that the obvious deployer countermeasure (augmenting training data with period prose) fails to close the vulnerability. Our findings show that the tested detector families, including adversarially fine-tuned ones, exhibit persistent vulnerabilities under structural out-of-distribution shifts, a mechanism that directly powers our leading competition performance.

</details>


### [150] [Protective Capacity Hallucination: When Large Language Models Claim Nonexistent Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13596)
*Eunna Lee,Jungpyo Nam,Sunjun Hwang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"保护能力幻觉"（PCH）现象，即LLM在被赋予保护者角色但无明确能力边界时，会声称自己能执行实际无法完成的保护行动（如联系紧急服务）。研究发现PCH由情境严重性和交互格式共同调控：多轮对话驱动其在大多数模型中达天花板水平，但在亲密伴侣冲突场景（有安全对齐覆盖）中，尽管物理严重性更高，PCH在所有8个模型中仍处于地板水平。作者将PCH解释为角色分配与能力边界规范之间的部署-设计鸿沟，是部分对齐的副产品——普遍训练的助人压力超越了领域选择性的帮助方式规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示LLM在扮演保护者角色时产生的自我指涉错误归因现象（PCH），即模型声称拥有超出其实际能力的物理或机构代理权，并探究其触发条件与根本原因。

Method: 三阶段研究，涵盖8个LLM和13,600个会话，系统操控情境严重性和交互格式（多对多对话 vs 其他格式），对比普通服务领域与安全对齐领域（亲密伴侣冲突）中的PCH表现。

Result: PCH受情境严重性和交互格式共同调控：多对多对话输入使大多数模型在普通服务领域达到天花板；但在安全对齐的亲密伴侣冲突场景中，尽管物理严重性更高，所有8个模型的PCH均处于地板水平。PCH的抑制程度与对齐覆盖范围相关，而非情境严重性。

Conclusion: PCH是部分对齐的副产品，源于普遍训练的助人压力与领域选择性帮助方式规范之间的不匹配。部署侧明确指定能力边界而非依赖对齐覆盖，是通用的缓解目标。

Abstract: When cast as the protector of a vulnerable user yet given no explicit capability boundary, a large language model (LLM) may respond not by acknowledging its limits but by claiming to have taken -- or to be taking -- a real-world protective action it cannot perform, such as contacting emergency services or administering care. We term this phenomenon Protective Capacity Hallucination (PCH): a self-referential misattribution in which a model, acting in a protective role, asserts physical or institutional agency exceeding its affordances as a language model. In a three-phase study spanning eight LLMs and 13{,}600 sessions, we find PCH jointly gated by situational severity and interactional format: multi-party dialogic input drives it toward ceiling in most models across ordinary service domains, whereas in intimate-partner conflict -- a domain explicitly covered by safety alignment -- it remains at floor in all eight models despite greater physical severity. We interpret PCH as the signature of a deployment-design gap between role assignment and capability-boundary specification: a by-product of partial alignment in which a universally trained pressure to help outruns a domain-selective specification of how to help. Because suppression tracks alignment coverage rather than severity, deployment-side specification of capability boundaries emerges as a general mitigation target.

</details>


### [151] [WarpGuard: Towards Control-Flow Attestation for Heterogeneous CPU-GPU Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13640)
*Christian Lindenmeier,Meni Orenbach,Amro Awad,Fabian Schwarz,Fritz Alder,Ahmad Atamli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WarpGuard是首个针对异构CPU-GPU工作负载的复合控制流证明框架，通过统一控制流图实现运行时GPU内核证明和CPU-GPU边界违规检测，在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上验证了其检测能力和适度开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制流证明技术仅关注CPU侧，GPU证明局限于静态加载时验证，无法提供运行时保证，导致GPU内核运行时攻击和CPU-GPU交互合约违反问题未被解决。

Method: WarpGuard构建统一的CPU-GPU控制流图，通过软件插桩实现对GPU内核的运行时追踪和内核特定CFG验证，同时监控内核启动事件并强制执行按调用站点策略以检测边界违规。

Result: 在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上的实验表明，WarpGuard能够检测GPU侧控制流攻击和跨边界攻击，在微基准测试、SPECAccel和8个TensorRT推理工作负载中开销适中。

Conclusion: WarpGuard作为首个复合CFA框架，有效填补了异构CPU-GPU系统运行时完整性证明的空白，证明了其在嵌入式安全关键场景中的实用性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous CPU-GPU workloads are increasingly used in safety-critical embedded systems, yet no existing approach provides joint attestation of their execution. Prior Control-Flow Attestation (CFA) techniques focus on CPU-side CFA, while GPU attestation is limited to static, load-time verification and does not provide runtime guarantees. As a result, runtime attacks on GPU kernels and violations of the CPU-GPU interaction contract remain unaddressed. We present WarpGuard, the first composite CFA framework for heterogeneous CPU-GPU workloads. WarpGuard verifies execution against a unified control-flow graph (CFG) that captures both CPU and GPU components. It extends prior CFA techniques in two ways: it enables runtime CFA of GPU kernels by tracing their execution against kernel-specific CFGs, and it monitors kernel launch events and enforces per-call site policies to detect violations at the CPU-GPU boundary. These extensions address challenges arising from GPU parallelism and cross-device interactions. We implement WarpGuard using software-based instrumentation, requiring no specialized hardware or binary modifications. Our evaluation on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano shows that WarpGuard detects GPU-side control-flow and cross-boundary attacks. Across microbenchmarks, SPECAccel, and eight TensorRT inference workloads, WarpGuard incurs moderate overheads, suggesting practicality for embedded safety-critical settings.

</details>


### [152] [How Agents Ask for Permission: User Permissions for AI Agents, from Interfaces to Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13718)
*Alexandra E. Michael,Franziska Roesner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过调研21个AI Agent权限系统提案，构建了用户级权限策略的分类体系，涵盖策略制定、推导和执行三个维度，并对比了5个主流商业Agent，发现现有系统在用户级权限支持上存在不足，指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Agent面临提示注入攻击、幻觉和未经授权执行敏感任务等安全风险；现有系统多采用产品级统一权限策略，未能满足不同用户的需求和偏好，亟需用户级权限策略支持。

Method: 对21个Agent权限系统提案进行综述，构建分类体系（用户界面/内部策略制定、从用户输入推导策略、运行时策略执行）；分析5个商业Agent并与文献系统对比。

Result: 形成了Agent权限系统的分类体系；识别了文献与商业系统之间的差距；归纳了多个高层次主题。

Conclusion: 用户级权限策略对AI Agent安全至关重要，当前系统支持不足，未来需在权限粒度、策略推导和运行时执行等方面进一步研究。

Abstract: As AI agents gain prevalance, users are increasingly exposed to the risks such systems entail. Prompt injection attacks, as well as hallucination, can cause agents to leak private information to third parties. As autonomous systems, agents also present the more active danger of performing sensitive tasks, such as bank transactions, without the user's intent or authorization.
  Recognizing this challenge, the agentic security community has developed numerous proposals for secure agentic systems. Much of this work has focused on product-level approaches, where agentic system developers determine and apply the same security policies and permissions to all users. Yet different users have different needs and preferences, necessitating support for user-level permissions policies in agentic AI systems.
  To understand how user-level permissions are handled in AI agent systems, we survey 21 proposals for agent permissions systems. From this review, we construct a taxonomy of how different systems specify user-level permissions policies, both at the user interface and internally; derive internal policies from user input; and enforce those policies at run-time. We then analyze five prominent commercial agents and compare their permissions handling to agentic permissions systems in the literature. We identify several high-level themes across the literature and commerical agents, as well as multiple gaps where future work is needed.

</details>


### [153] [PriEval-Protect: A Unified Framework for Privacy Evaluation and Protection in Healthcare Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13754)
*Ilef Chebil,Asma El Hadj,Souheib Yousfi,Aroua Hedhili,Layth Sliman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了PriEval-Protect两阶段框架，结合微调法律LLM与RAG进行法规合规评分，以及加密类型、数据架构和多种指标的技术分析，通过层次分析法加权聚合生成综合风险评分，并基于评估结果推荐联邦学习和差分隐私等缓解措施，实现了从法规合规到数据级风险分析的统一隐私评估与保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私合规方法依赖人工，容易出错，且将政策审计与数据级评估分离，无法实现统一的风险分析。

Method: 提出PriEval-Protect两阶段框架：评估阶段使用微调法律LLM配合RAG进行法规合规评分，并结合加密类型、数据架构、相似性、不确定性、攻击者成功率和信息增益/损失等指标进行技术分析，通过层次分析法加权聚合为综合风险评分；保护阶段基于评估结果推荐联邦学习和差分隐私等缓解措施。

Result: 在医院文档和数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够生成与法规对齐、可解释的评估结果，有效连接法律合规性与数据级风险分析。

Conclusion: PriEval-Protect实现了法规合规与数据级风险分析的统一框架，提供了可解释的评估结果，为GDPR和HIPAA下的患者隐私保护提供了自动化、系统化的解决方案。

Abstract: Safeguarding patient privacy while enabling meaningful healthcare data use remains critical under GDPR and HIPAA. Existing compliance methods are manual, error-prone, and separate policy audits from data-level assessments. This paper presents PriEval-Protect, a two-phase framework for unified privacy risk evaluation and mitigation. The evaluation phase combines regulatory compliance scoring using a fine-tuned legal LLM with RAG, and technical analysis via encryption type, data architecture, and metrics including similarity, uncertainty, adversary success, and information gain/loss. A composite risk score uses weighted aggregation via Analytic Hierarchy Process. The protection phase recommends countermeasures including federated learning and differential privacy based on assessed risk. Results on hospital documents and datasets demonstrate regulation-aligned, explainable assessments, bridging legal conformance and data-level risk analysis.

</details>


### [154] [Traffic-Aware Randomized Smoothing for LLM-Based Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13801)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对LLM入侵检测系统的认证防御方法TA-RS，通过对直接可控特征子空间添加高斯噪声来提供可认证的鲁棒性保证，实验表明在多个数据集上取得55-100%的认证准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的入侵检测系统容易受到流量操纵攻击，现有研究主要依赖经验性评估，缺乏形式化的鲁棒性保证。需要一种可认证的防御方法，确保即使攻击者操纵部分网络流量特征，IDS仍能做出正确检测。

Method: 提出Traffic-Aware Randomized Smoothing (TA-RS)，一种分类器无关的认证防御方法。核心思想是仅在攻击者可直接控制的特征子空间（DC子空间）上添加高斯噪声，在微调和认证阶段保持一致，使平滑分布与攻击者可控子空间对齐。使用L_inf等效阈值R_inf = epsilon*sqrt(|DC|)进行评估。

Result: (1) 在CIC-IDS-2018和HIKARI-2021上达到55-100%认证准确率；(2) 中位认证半径超过R_inf 1.8-5倍；(3) 相比训练等价的RS基线，优势为4-19 pp；(4) 相比各向同性RS基线，优势高达72 pp，但主要源于训练-认证不匹配；(5) RT-IoT2022上默认方案失败，增加噪声增强后恢复至76%/69%。

Conclusion: TA-RS能够为LLM入侵检测系统提供有效的可认证鲁棒性，但效果依赖于数据集特性、基础模型选择和噪声增强策略的合理配置。方法的局限性在于无法处理完全不受控制的特征扰动，且在某些数据集上需要调整训练策略。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based intrusion detection systems (IDS) are increasingly studied for security monitoring, yet their robustness against feasible traffic manipulation remains largely empirical. We present Traffic-Aware Randomized Smoothing (TA-RS), a classifier-agnostic certified defense that injects Gaussian noise exclusively into the directly controllable (DC) subspace -- features a remote attacker can modify -- during both fine-tuning and certification, aligning the smoothing distribution with the attacker-controllable subspace. We identify a critical prerequisite: applying standard randomized smoothing to clean-trained LLM-IDS yields weak certified accuracy in three of four (model, dataset) pairs tested (14-33%, at or below random) and only 57% in the fourth (43 pp below the noise-augmented result); noise-augmented fine-tuning recovers to 68-100% on two of three benchmark datasets (at sigma=0.25). At the L_inf-equivalent threshold R_inf = epsilon*sqrt(|DC|) (epsilon=0.05), TA-RS achieves 55-100% certified accuracy on CIC-IDS-2018 and HIKARI-2021, with median certified radii (R approx 0.45-0.96) exceeding R_inf by 1.8-5x (across sigma=0.25-1.00). Against a fairly trained iso-trained RS baseline the residual advantage is dataset-dependent (4-19 pp on CIC-IDS-2018). The larger gap -- up to 72 pp against an isotropic RS baseline that shares the DC-noise-augmented training recipe -- primarily reflects the training-certification mismatch rather than DC alignment alone: isotropic test-time noise perturbs uncontrollable features the attacker cannot exploit, triggering abstention rates up to 68%. RT-IoT2022 probes the limits of the method: it fails under the default fine-tuning recipe but recovers to 76%/69% certified accuracy (LLaMA3-8B/Qwen3-8B) when noise augmentation is increased.

</details>


### [155] [Assessing the Forensic Viability of Android Memory Analysis Across Production Builds: A Cross-Version Study of Security Hardening and Structure Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13821)
*Jayasimha Nannapanen,Sneha Sudhakaran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文测量了Android 8至15版本中libart.so调试符号被剥离的程度，发现静态符号从20,495降至零，动态符号下降约60%，但底层内存架构（堆空间、GC基础设施、分配位图）在结构上仍保持完整，可通过版本匹配的开发构建偏移量在完整剥离的生产设备上定位运行时入口点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前没有研究量化谷歌在消费者手机上剥离libart.so调试符号的具体进展，也没有验证剥离后的内存架构是否仍符合取证文献的描述。这导致取证工具依赖的符号信息与实际设备严重脱节。

Method: 从Pixel工厂镜像提取Android 8至15的libart.so二进制文件，统计静态符号、动态符号和源文件引用的数量变化；通过源代码审查和内存映射对比分析堆空间、GC基础设施、分配位图的结构完整性；在已root的完全剥离Pixel 7上进行现场验证，测试动态符号表定位运行时入口点的可行性及开发构建偏移量的有效性。

Result: 静态符号从20,495降为零，动态符号下降约60%，源文件引用完全消失；Android 15中新增的压缩回退段恢复了数千个函数名但不包含结构布局；核心内存结构在Android 8和15之间保持完整，变化仅限于命名和算术优化；现场验证表明动态符号表仍可定位运行时入口点，版本匹配的开发构建偏移量在完整剥离的生产内存中能解出有效指针。

Conclusion: 尽管谷歌大幅剥离了调试符号，但Android运行时的底层内存架构在结构上并未改变，取证分析仍可通过版本匹配的开发构建偏移量在完全剥离的生产设备上定位关键数据结构。这为未来取证工具在符号缺失环境下继续发挥作用提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Android memory forensics recovers evidence that never touches disk: decrypted messages, session credentials, and the live internal state of a running application. The tools that perform this recovery depend on debug symbols embedded in libart.so, the Android Runtime library, to locate data structures and interpret their layout. Across recent releases Google has stripped most of that information from the binaries that ship on consumer phones as part of a broader security hardening effort, yet no prior work has measured how far the stripping has progressed on the devices examiners actually encounter, or whether the memory architecture beneath the stripped surface still resembles what the forensics literature describes. This paper measures the gap between the unstripped development builds researchers use and the production builds that ship on real hardware, using binaries extracted from Pixel factory images across Android 8 through Android 15. Static symbols fell from 20,495 entries to zero, dynamic symbols dropped by roughly 60 percent, and source file references disappeared entirely. A compressed fallback section in the Android 15 binary restores thousands of function names but carries no structure layouts. Source code review and memory map comparisons across Android 8 and 15 show that the heap spaces, garbage collector infrastructure, and allocation bitmaps remain structurally intact, with visible changes limited to naming and arithmetic optimization. Live validation on a rooted, fully stripped Pixel 7 confirms that the runtime entry point is still locatable through the dynamic symbol table, and that structural offsets pulled from a version-matched development build resolve to valid pointers inside production memory.

</details>


### [156] [Reveal, Correct, Then Pay: Encrypted Mempools and Perpetual Funding Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13832)
*Benjamin Marsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究加密内存池中自作者状态操纵攻击，以永续合约资金费率为典型场景，揭示隐私机制造成的"经济反应缺口"——即使解密开销可忽略，承诺-揭示流程仍会阻碍适应性修正交易进入已确认批次，导致修正率低于自适应水平，并通过反应因子和资本化屏蔽双重渠道放大攻击收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注受害者依赖型MEV（如三明治攻击），但加密内存池可能引入新型攻击：攻击者利用自身交易的公开知识和事后状态索赔权，在隐私保护造成的反应缺口中获利。永续合约资金费率是典型场景，攻击者既是交易发起者又是费率接收方，形成自作者状态操纵。

Method: 将修正行为建模为可执行套利机会，修正者根据局部价格冲击和库存成本选择订单规模；协议信息调度决定哪些机会可操作。通过比较"封闭阶段"（承诺后-揭示前）与"自适应阶段"（揭示后）的修正率，推导反应因子，量化隐私造成的修正缺口。

Result: 1）排序壁垒移除了自适应搜索者，导致封闭阶段修正率低于揭示后的自适应修正率；2）进入资金窗口的扭曲被显式反应因子放大；3）交易隐私降低可预测资金费率在入场价格中的资本化程度，形成第二放大渠道；4）构建本地安全指标，区分攻击者盲区、修正屏蔽和资本化屏蔽三种效应。

Conclusion: 加密内存池在消除某些MEV的同时，会引入自作者状态操纵风险，其损害通过反应因子和资本化屏蔽双重渠道放大。这为去中心化金融协议设计交易排序和隐私机制提供了安全权衡分析框架。

Abstract: Encrypted mempools are designed to hide transaction contents until execution order is fixed, preventing many victim dependent forms of maximal extractable value. This paper studies a different class of attack in the form of self-authored state manipulation, in which the attacker knows its own transaction and owns a downstream claim on the state that transaction changes. Perpetual futures funding is a canonical example. The funding signal determines a transfer rate, while receiving side open interest is the transfer base. In a commit then reveal mempool, an adaptive corrective transaction cannot enter the already committed batch. Privacy can therefore create an economic reaction gap even when cryptographic decryption overhead is negligible. We microfound correction through executable arbitrage opportunities. Correctors choose order size against local price impact and inventory cost, while the protocol information schedule determines which opportunities are actionable. The ordering barrier removes ordinary adaptive searchers from the closed stage. It therefore yields a closed stage correction rate below the adaptive correction rate whenever positive adaptive capacity becomes available after reveal. The distortion entering a funding window is multiplied by an explicit response factor. Transaction privacy can also reduce capitalization of predictable funding into entry prices, producing a second amplification channel. The resulting local security index separates attacker blindness, correction shielding, and capitalization shielding.

</details>


### [157] [From Classification to Consistent Templates: Multiple Permuted-Label Classifier Encoding for Biometric Template Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13845)
*Baogang Song,Zhongshu Zhao,Qianrong Zheng,Jianwen Xiang,Dongdong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MPLCE方法，通过多个排列标签分类器将生物特征样本映射到身份级输出，再经编码、异或混淆和哈希处理，实现精确匹配的模板保护，无需纠错码或辅助数据，在面部和虹膜数据集上取得高识别率，并满足不可逆、可撤销和不可链接性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物特征模板保护方法依赖可比较的相似度匹配、纠错码或预定义映射，存在相似结构泄露、辅助数据风险、依赖人工目标或与特定模态耦合等问题。纯哈希精确匹配要求生成完全相同的中介模板，身份分类天然可将可变样本映射到稳定输出，因此探索基于分类的哈希保护方案。

Method: 提出MPLCE，为每个分类器使用不同的标签排列，将同一身份在不同分类器中分配不同标签；编码并拼接预测标签形成中介模板，扩大有效候选空间；使用应用特定XOR字符串随机化模板，再进行密码学哈希，实现精确匹配验证，无需纠错码或生物特征依赖的辅助数据。

Result: 在四个面部和两个虹膜数据集上，YTF数据集上FAR为5.51×10⁻⁵%时GAR达98.61%，CASIA-Iris-Lamp上FAR为0.00%时GAR达99.10%。安全性分析和攻击评估支持其不可逆性、可撤销性和不可链接性。

Conclusion: MPLCE通过排列标签分类器编码和哈希保护，在保持竞争性识别性能的同时，实现了跨模态一致的模板保护，无需纠错码或辅助数据，具备良好的安全性属性。

Abstract: Biometric template protection (BTP) must secure stored templates while tolerating intra-class variations. Existing methods rely on protected-domain similarity matching, error correction, or predefined-template mappings, potentially retaining exploitable similarity structures, introducing helper-data risks, depending on artificial targets, or coupling protection to specific modalities. Storing only cryptographic hash digests eliminates directly comparable representations and conceals pre-hash templates, but hash-based exact-match verification requires genuine samples to generate identical intermediate templates before hashing. Identity classification is naturally suited to this requirement because it maps variable biometric samples to stable and discriminative identity-level outputs. Based on this insight, we propose Multiple Permuted-Label Classifier Encoding (MPLCE). Through classifier-specific label permutations, MPLCE assigns each identity different labels across multiple classifiers. The predicted labels are encoded and concatenated to form an intermediate template, preventing repeated encodings of a single identity label and enlarging the effective candidate space while preserving classification consistency. The template is randomized with an application-specific XOR string and cryptographically hashed, enabling exact-match verification without error correction codes or biometric-dependent helper data. Using modality-specific classifiers, MPLCE retains the same template generation and protection procedure across modalities. On four face and two iris datasets, MPLCE achieves competitive performance, including a GAR of 98.61\% at a FAR of 5.51\(\times\)10\textsuperscript{-5}\% on YTF and a GAR of 99.10\% at a FAR of 0.00\% on CASIA-Iris-Lamp. Security analyses and attack evaluations support its irreversibility, revocability, and unlinkability under the threat model.

</details>


### [158] [Plausible Deniability Guarantees for Whistleblowers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13928)
*Leo Richter,Matt J. Kusner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文形式化了举报人保护中的差分隐私威胁模型，证明了随机响应方法在审计选择中无法优于均匀随机审计，并提出了一种基于持续计数机制的通用私有审计方案，实现了每报告$(0,δ)$-差分隐私且噪声规模为$O(\sqrt{\log T})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 举报人面临报复威胁而不敢举报，现有保护方案缺乏正式隐私保证，且差分隐私机制未针对被审计组织本身观察审计选择并据此识别举报人的自然威胁模型。

Method: 将强敌手威胁模型形式化为审计记录上的每报告$(0,δ)$-差分隐私；证明随机响应无法显著优于均匀随机审计；将私有审计归约到私有持续计数问题，任何$(0,δ)$-DP持续计数器可通过后处理嵌入审计流程。

Result: 基于持续计数实例化得到每报告$(0,δ)$-DP，噪声规模$O(\sqrt{\log T})$；当被举报最多组织与次多组织的报告差距增长快于$\sqrt{\log T}$时，选择误差消失；仿真显示该方法显著优于随机响应。

Conclusion: 提出了一种具有形式化差分隐私保证的举报人保护机制，在理论保证和实际效用上均优于现有方案。

Abstract: Whistleblowers are a key safeguard against organizational wrongdoing, but the threat of retaliation deters reporting. Existing whistleblower-protection proposals lack formal privacy guarantees, and existing differential privacy mechanisms do not directly target the natural threat model -- one in which the audited organization itself observes auditor selection decisions and uses them to identify reporters. We formalize protection against a strong-adversary threat model as per-report $(0, δ)$-differential privacy on the transcript of audit selections. Within this framework we prove that a natural approach -- randomized response applied at the selection step -- can never outperform uniform random auditing by more than $δ$ at any horizon. We then give a generic mechanism that reduces private auditing to private continual counting: any $(0, δ)$-DP continual counter plugs in by post-processing, and the audit transcript inherits the same per-report guarantee. Instantiating the reduction with a recent work in continual counting yields per-report $(0, δ)$-DP with noise scaling as $O(\sqrt{\log T})$ across a horizon of $T$ audit decisions. A utility theorem shows that the selection error vanishes whenever the noisy report gap between the most-reported organization and the runner-up grows faster than $\sqrt{\log T}$. Simulations show a substantial improvement over randomized response.

</details>


### [159] [HORCRUX: A Complete PQC RISC-V eXtension Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13939)
*Alessandra Dolmeta,Valeria Piscopo,Michael Hutter,Maurizio Martina,Guido Masera*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HORCRUX，一个紧凑的RISC-V扩展指令集，统一支持所有NIST批准的PQC算法，在FPGA上实现最高129倍加速，资源开销少于21k LUTs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件扩展通常只支持有限的PQC子集，难以在密码敏捷性、高性能和低资源消耗之间取得平衡，尤其缺乏面向资源受限嵌入式系统的统一解决方案。

Method: 通过分析ML-KEM、ML-DSA、SLH-DSA、HQC和Falcon的共享计算内核，设计统一的指令集扩展，并由资源高效的紧耦合协处理器执行。

Result: 在Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA上，哈希类方案加速比达129x，格基方案9x，编码基方案27x；资源开销<21k LUTs和<4.4k FFs；提供65nm CMOS的综合后ASIC结果及功耗表征。

Conclusion: HORCRUX模块化架构与标准RISC-V核向后兼容，为资源受限的嵌入式系统部署PQC提供了可扩展的高效解决方案。

Abstract: This work presents a compact RISC-V extension for Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) called HORCRUX, which provides a unified Instruction-Set Extension (ISE) supporting all NIST-approved PQC algorithms. HORCRUX addresses the difficult trade-off between crypto-agility, high performance, and low resource consumption in constrained environments, a balance typically missing in hardware extensions that focus on limited PQC subsets. By targeting shared kernels across ML-KEM, MLDSA, SLH-DSA, HQC, and Falcon, the extension introduces new RISC-V instructions executed by a resource-efficient, tightly coupled coprocessor. This architecture is specifically optimized for embedded systems with strict energy budgets and limited area. Experimental evaluation on a Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA demonstrates speedups of up to 129x for hash-based, 9x for lattice-based, and 27x for code-based schemes, while adding fewer than 21k LUTs and 4.4k FFs. ASIC results from postsynthesis characterization in 65 nm CMOS are also reported, alongside a rigorous power characterization to validate the architecture's energy efficiency. The extension's modular structure maintains backward compatibility with standard RISC-V cores, offering a scalable solution for deploying PQC on constrained embedded systems.

</details>


### [160] [Agent Skill Security: Threat Models, Attacks, Defenses, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13987)
*Sanket Badhe,Priyanka Tiwari*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillSec-Eval，一个面向可复用智能体技能生命周期安全评估的框架，通过对327个真实技能的实证分析，发现除运行时执行外，在技能入库、语义检索、规划器选择及技能演化等阶段均存在安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全研究主要集中在提示注入和运行时执行，忽略了可复用技能在整个生命周期（从入库到演化）中更广泛的安全风险，缺乏系统性的安全评估方法。

Method: 首先刻画技能生命周期，建立涵盖仓库准入、语义检索、规划器选择、执行和技能演化的威胁分类体系；然后基于该分类体系实例化SkillSec-Eval框架，并在包含327个真实技能的仓库上进行全面的实证评估。

Result: 实验表明，漏洞不仅存在于执行阶段，在生命周期的多个阶段（入库、检索、选择、演化）均存在安全风险，揭示了生命周期感知安全分析的必要性。

Conclusion: 可复用智能体技能的安全评估需要从生命周期视角出发，覆盖执行以外的多个阶段，SkillSec-Eval为此提供了系统的评估框架和实证基础。

Abstract: Reusable skills are becoming a fundamental building block of Large Language Model (LLM) agents, enabling capabilities to be packaged, shared, and reused across diverse applications. However, existing security research primarily focuses on prompt injection and runtime execution, leaving security risks throughout the broader skill lifecycle largely unexplored. In this paper, we present SkillSec-Eval, a lifecycle-aware framework for systematically evaluating the security of reusable agent skills. We first characterize the skill lifecycle and develop a threat taxonomy spanning repository admission, semantic retrieval, planner selection, execution, and skill evolution. We then instantiate this taxonomy in SkillSec-Eval and conduct a comprehensive empirical evaluation using a repository of 327 real-world skills. Our study demonstrates that vulnerabilities arise at multiple lifecycle stages beyond execution, highlighting the need for lifecycle-aware security analysis of reusable agent skills.

</details>


### [161] [Rethinking Penetration Testing for AI-Enabled Systems: From Resource Compromise to Behavioral Objective Violation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14006)
*Mohammad Allahbakhsh,Mohammad Hassan Bahari,Moslem Attar-Raouf*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文重新定义了AI系统的渗透测试：从传统的基础设施突破转向以目标为导向的行为评估，并提出了包含识别操作目标、映射AI控制行为、分析对抗影响面、定义行为失败标准、执行场景测试和报告证据的六步工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统渗透测试评估对手能否利用软件、基础设施、配置或操作控制中的弱点实现安全攻破，但这种范式对于AI系统已不再充分——对手可通过影响提示词、检索内容、传感器输入、训练数据、记忆、工具或人机交互循环来改变系统行为，而无需直接攻破底层基础设施。

Method: 定义AI系统为学习模型实质性影响操作结果的系统；定义AI渗透为在明确威胁模型下，诱导AI控制行为违反一个或多个操作目标的可行方式。提出六步工作流：1) 识别操作目标，2) 映射AI控制行为，3) 分析对抗影响面，4) 定义行为失败标准，5) 执行基于场景的测试，6) 报告将对抗行为与目标违规联系起来的证据。

Result: 该框架保留了传统渗透测试，同时将其扩展至提示注入、间接提示注入、数据投毒、传感器操纵、检索投毒、工具误用和智能体失调等对抗路径。通过AI驱动的安全运营中心助手示例说明如何通过行为影响而非基础设施攻破实现渗透。

Conclusion: 所提出的定义、工作流和示例为评估已部署AI系统中对抗成功的程度提供了技术框架。

Abstract: Penetration testing traditionally evaluates whether adversaries can exploit weaknesses in software, infrastructure, configurations, or operational controls to achieve security-relevant compromise. This paradigm remains necessary for AI-enabled systems, but it is no longer sufficient. In such systems, adversaries may influence prompts, retrieved content, sensor inputs, training data, memory, tools, or human-AI interaction loops to alter system behavior without directly compromising the underlying infrastructure. This paper reframes penetration testing for AI-enabled systems as objective-driven behavioral evaluation. We define an AI-enabled system as one in which learned models materially influence behavior affecting operational outcomes, and we define AI-enabled penetration as the feasible induction of AI-governed behavior that violates one or more operational objectives under an explicit threat model. This definition preserves conventional penetration testing while extending it to adversarial pathways such as prompt injection, indirect prompt injection, data poisoning, sensor manipulation, retrieval poisoning, tool misuse, and agentic misalignment. We further propose a testing workflow that identifies operational objectives, maps AI-governed behavior, analyzes adversarial influence surfaces, defines behavioral failure criteria, executes scenario-based tests, and reports evidence linking adversarial action to objective violation. A running example involving an AI-enabled security operations center assistant illustrates how penetration may occur through behavioral influence rather than infrastructure compromise. Together, the definitions, workflow, and example provide a technical framework for evaluating adversarial success in deployed AI-enabled systems.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Price of Fairness in Bandits: A Tight Minimax Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13402)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Soumyadeep Dutta,Sayak Ray Chowdhury*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了严格公平约束下的bandit问题，通过提出UCB-HARE算法并证明其下界，揭示了公平性代价与参数q的精确多项式关系：当q>1时，k^{q/2}的惩罚是信息论意义上不可避免的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准遗憾最小化算法将探索视为摊销成本，在临床试验等场景中可能导致早期参与者遭受不公平的期望损失。已有工作通过广义p-均值框架来评估公平性，但严格公平区域（q=-p>0）的紧界尚未解决，现有算法依赖于均匀早期探索，且不清楚对k的额外依赖是否本质性。

Method: 1) 使用needle-in-haystack构造证明算法无关的下界Ω(σ√(k^{max(1,q)}/T))；2) 提出UCB-HARE (Harmonic Anchored Rank Exploration)算法，用逆加权谐波排名调度替代均匀探索，并受认证的正均值锚点保护。

Result: 下界证明显示：对于q>1，额外依赖k^{q/2}是信息论不可避免的。UCB-HARE算法达到Õ(σ√(k^{max(1,q)}/T))的遗憾，与下界匹配至对数因子。合成实验证实UCB-HARE优于均匀探索基线，且增益随q增大而增加。

Conclusion: 该工作严格刻画了严格公平约束下bandit问题的精确多项式代价，闭合了现有理论空白，证明了公平性对探索策略的本质影响。

Abstract: In bandit problems, standard regret-minimizing algorithms treat exploration as an amortized cost, which can expose early participants to unfair ex-ante losses in settings such as clinical trials. Recent work addresses this by evaluating the sequence of per-round expected rewards through the generalized $p$-mean, interpolating between utilitarian welfare ($p=1$), Nash welfare ($p\to0$), and Rawlsian fairness ($p\to-\infty$). Although tight guarantees are known for $p\ge0$, the strictly fair regime $q=-p>0$ remains unresolved because negative-power means are dominated by the smallest per-round rewards.
  For $σ$-sub-Gaussian rewards with nonnegative means, the best prior algorithm relied on uniform early exploration and achieved regret $O(k^{(q+1)/2}/\sqrt{T})$, while the only general lower bound was the classical $Ω(σ\sqrt{k/T})$. Thus it was unclear whether the extra dependence on $k$ was intrinsic to strict fairness or an artifact of uniform exploration.
  We close this gap by identifying the exact polynomial price of strict fairness. Using a needle-in-haystack construction, we prove an algorithm-independent lower bound $Ω(σ\sqrt{k^{\max(1,q)}/T})$; for $q>1$, this shows that the penalty $k^{q/2}$ is information-theoretically unavoidable. We then introduce \textsf{UCB-HARE} (Harmonic Anchored Rank Exploration), which replaces uniform exploration with an inverse-weighted harmonic rank schedule protected by a certified positive-mean anchor. Its regret is $\widetilde{O}(σ\sqrt{k^{\max(1,q)}/T})$, matching the lower bound up to logarithmic factors. Experiments on synthetic instances confirm that \textsf{UCB-HARE} improves over uniform-exploration baselines, with gains increasing as $q$ grows.

</details>


### [163] [Non-Expansive Two-Time-Scale Stochastic Approximation: A Fixed-Schedule One-Quarter Barrier and Bias-Corrected Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13414)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究非扩张双时间尺度随机逼近问题，建立了KM残差的下界，并通过残差预条件慢速预言机和嵌套Tikhonov-KM算法将收敛率从T^{-1/4+o(1)}提升至T^{-1/3+o(1)}，最终单循环算法达到T^{-1/2+o(1)}的收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双时间尺度随机逼近理论主要集中在压缩映射情形，而非扩张（Krasnoselskii--Mann型）情形下的收敛行为尚不清晰，此前仅观察到k^{-1/4+o(1)}的末次迭代均方残差指数，缺乏理论下界解释。

Method: 1) 证明有限时域下界，表明KM残差尺度的最坏情况紧性；2) 提出残差预条件慢速预言机，消除快速跟踪误差的一阶依赖；3) 设计嵌套Tikhonov-KM算法（未校正/校正预言机）；4) 提出单循环算法，在线跟踪快速平衡和泄漏预条件子。

Result: 未校正预言机：T^{-1/4+o(1)}；校正预言机：T^{-1/3+o(1)}；单循环算法：T^{-1/2+o(1)}，每次迭代仅需O(1)原始样本。

Conclusion: 通过残差预条件技术将慢速预言机偏差从一阶降为二阶，显著提升收敛率；单循环算法避免了嵌套方法的内循环重复代价，达到最优收敛速率。

Abstract: Non-expansive two-time-scale stochastic approximation is governed by a slow stochastic Krasnoselskii--Mann fixed-point iteration rather than by contraction to a unique equilibrium. We study this regime under a contractive fast map and a non-expansive reduced slow map. We first prove a finite-horizon lower bound showing that, for any prescribed slow stepsize schedule $(β_k)$, the classical KM residual scale $(\sum_{i<N}β_i(1-β_i))^{-1}$ is worst-case sharp for the corresponding unregularized KM update. Combined with the raw fast-tracking leakage scale, this explains the previously observed $k^{-1/4+o(1)}$ last-iterate mean-square residual exponent.
  We then introduce a residual-preconditioned slow oracle that cancels the first-order dependence on the fast tracking error. In a nested Tikhonov-KM algorithm, the uncorrected oracle yields total-sample rate $T^{-1/4+o(1)}$, while the corrected oracle yields $T^{-1/3+o(1)}$. This improvement comes from changing the slow-oracle bias from first order to second order in the fast error after all inner-loop samples are counted.
  Finally, we show that the repeated inner-loop cost of the nested method can be avoided in a smooth derivative-oracle model. A single-loop algorithm that tracks both the fast equilibrium and the leakage preconditioner online achieves $T^{-1/2+o(1)}$ with $O(1)$ primitive samples per iteration.

</details>


### [164] [Parallel gradient boosting for flexible estimation of conditional distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13550)
*Rémy Chapelle,Nicolas Vayatis,Bruno Falissard,Mohammed Sedki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出并行梯度提升算法，通过使用公共下降方向，每次迭代只需训练一个基模型即可处理多目标输出（如多分位数回归），在保持与XGBoost相当预测质量的同时，速度提升数个数量级，尤其在高维、混合/缺失协变量场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统梯度提升扩展到多输出预测问题时，每个目标在每个迭代都需要训练一个基模型，计算成本高；现有高效变体通常局限于特定损失函数和基学习器（如决策树）。本文旨在设计一种通用、高效的提升方法，突破上述限制。

Method: 提出并行梯度提升算法，核心思想是对所有训练观测使用公共下降方向，使得每轮迭代仅需一个基模型，无论目标数量多少。建立了算法收敛的充分条件，并将其应用于多分位数回归场景。

Result: 在多分位数回归设置中，该方法提供与XGBoost等最先进库相当的预测质量，但速度提升数个数量级。在高维、混合/缺失协变量场景下，条件分布估计器优于其他非参数和半参数估计器。

Conclusion: 并行梯度提升是一种高效、通用的多输出预测方法，显著降低了计算开销，同时保持预测性能，尤其适用于复杂数据场景。

Abstract: Boosting is one of the most successful learning techniques for standard classification and regression tasks. Its extension to multi-output prediction problems has found an increasing number of applications in recent years. Among them is the prediction of entire conditional distributions rather than single functionals, which can often be framed as a multi-output regression problem, for example multiple quantile regression. Addressing such problems with classical implementations of boosting is computationally challenging, because usually one base model is trained for each target at every iteration. More efficient variants of boosting have been proposed to speed up training, but they tend to be tied to specific loss functions and classes of base learners, usually decision trees. In this work, we study a modification of the gradient boosting algorithm, which we call parallel gradient boosting, designed to circumvent all these limitations. The core idea is to use a common descent direction for all training observations. By doing so, only one base model is needed at each iteration, regardless of the number of targets, which allows for considerable performance gains. We establish sufficient conditions for the convergence of the algorithm, whose practical use is introduced via the multiple quantile regression setting. We show that in such a setting, it provides predictions of similar quality to state-of-the-art boosting libraries such as XGBoost, while being faster by several orders of magnitude. Then, we evaluate the properties of the resulting conditional distribution estimator, which is shown empirically to outperform other nonparametric and semiparametric estimators, especially in high-dimensional settings and in the presence of mixed and/or missing covariates.

</details>


### [165] [Multimodal Empirical Bayes Variational Autoencoders for Joint Longitudinal and Time-to-Event Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13984)
*Anders Sjöberg,Nils Olsson,Marcus Baaz,Mats Jirstrand*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将经验贝叶斯变分自编码器（EB-VAE）扩展至纵向肿瘤测量与时间-事件联合建模框架，通过混合解码器整合遗传协变量与丢失信息，在黑色素瘤和乳腺癌数据上验证了其预测性能与生物学合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向肿瘤测量、丢失信息与遗传协变量提供了互补的治疗反应信息，但现有群体建模框架难以有效整合这些异构数据源。

Method: 扩展EB-VAE框架，用协变量条件经验贝叶斯先验正则化潜变量个体效应，解码器映射至肿瘤体积轨迹；以风险模型增强解码器处理信息性丢失，采用全神经与混合半机制两种解码器形式，并通过基因条件先验适应整合基因组协变量。

Result: 混合解码器恢复的治疗效应参数与非线性混合效应估计一致；联合模型准确复现了留出个体的肿瘤体积分布与丢失模式；基因条件化改善了个体水平先验预测；稳定性选择识别出BRAF、NRAS、NF1与MDM2等生物学可信的遗传标志物。

Conclusion: EB-VAE为药物计量学应用提供了灵活的联合建模框架，能有效整合神经动力学、机制结构、时间-事件建模与高维协变量。

Abstract: Longitudinal tumor measurements, dropout information, and genetic covariates provide complementary information about treatment response, but integrating these data sources within a single population modeling framework remains challenging. We extend the empirical Bayes variational autoencoder (EB-VAE) framework to joint longitudinal and time-to-event modeling and evaluate it on tumor growth data. The framework represents inter-individual variability using latent individual effects regularized by a covariate-conditioned empirical Bayes prior, while a decoder maps these latent effects to tumor-volume trajectories. To account for informative dropout, the decoder was augmented with a hazard model, yielding joint predictions of tumor growth and time to dropout. We further compared fully neural and hybrid semi-mechanistic decoder formulations and incorporated genomic covariates through a genetics-conditioned prior adaptation. The hybrid decoder recovered treatment-effect parameters broadly consistent with previously reported nonlinear mixed-effects estimates, while achieving prior predictive performance comparable to the neural decoder. The joint model reproduced both tumor-volume distributions and dropout patterns in held-out individuals, and genetic conditioning improved individual-level prior predictions in both cutaneous melanoma and breast cancer experiments. Stability selection identified several biologically plausible genetic indicators, including alterations in BRAF, NRAS, NF1, and MDM2. These results demonstrate that EB-VAE provides a flexible probabilistic framework for combining neural dynamics, mechanistic structure, time-to-event modeling, and high-dimensional covariates in pharmacometric applications.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [On Rates Attainable under Random Design: A Negative Answer to a Problem of Robins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13170)
*P. M. Aronow,Patrick Lopatto*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文否定了James Robins关于非参数回归中常数条件方差估计的一个猜想。当回归函数为s-Hölder光滑、设计密度有界、条件误差方差恒定且四阶矩有界时，对于s>1且维度d>4s，极小极大均方根误差的下界为n^{-β}，其中β=d(3s+1)+8s/((d+2s)(d+4))，因此猜想的最优收敛速率n^{-4s/(d+4s)}并非一致可达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: James Robins提出了一个关于非参数回归中常数条件方差估计的问题，即是否存在一个统一可达的收敛速率。本文旨在给出这一问题的否定回答。

Method: 采用极小极大框架，在随机设计下，假设回归函数属于s-Hölder类，设计密度有上下界，条件误差均值为零、方差恒定且四阶矩一致有界。通过构造严格难以区分的参数子集，推导出均方根误差的下界。

Result: 在s>1且d>4s的条件下，极小极大均方根风险的下界为n^{-β}，其中β=d(3s+1)+8s/((d+2s)(d+4))。该速率低于Robins猜想的n^{-4s/(d+4s)}，表明后一速率并非一致可达。

Conclusion: Robins关于常数条件方差估计的最优收敛速率猜想在一般条件下不成立，特别是在高维且光滑度有限的情形下，可达速率更慢。

Abstract: We give a negative answer to a problem posed by James Robins on estimating a constant conditional variance in nonparametric regression under random design. For every $s>1$ and integer $d>4s$, when the regression function is $s$-Hölder, the unknown design density is bounded above and away from zero, and the conditional error laws may depend on the design but have mean zero, a common variance, and uniformly bounded fourth moments, we show that the minimax root-mean-square risk is bounded below by $n^{-β}$ with $β=\frac{d(3s+1)+8s}{(d+2s)(d+4)}$. Hence the conjectured rate $n^{-4s/(d+4s)}$ is not uniformly attainable.

</details>


### [167] [Overlapping window tests for correlation and trend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13173)
*Abbas Alhakim*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个通用框架，用于构建和分析重叠滑动窗口统计量以检测依赖性和趋势。通过将重叠块视为马尔可夫链，利用其协方差算子的谱结构对窗口函数空间进行正交分解，得到不同重叠层次的增量依赖信息，并给出了提取这些分量的显式过程。应用于相关检测（对称多项式窗口函数）和趋势检测（局部秩统计量），揭示了不同统计量可能具有不同的局部分辨尺度，包括非经典尺度。该框架为设计重叠窗口检验、推导渐近正态化和局部行为提供了系统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重叠滑动窗口方法缺乏统一的数学框架来系统分析窗口统计量的渐近性质、增量信息结构以及不同重叠层次对检测能力的影响。本文旨在填补这一空白，为依赖性和趋势检测提供严格的理论基础。

Method: 1) 将固定窗口大小的重叠块建模为马尔可夫链；2) 利用协方差算子的谱结构，对窗口函数空间进行正交分解，得到不同重叠层次的分量；3) 提出增量依赖信息的自然定义及提取过程；4) 在相关检测中研究对称多项式窗口函数，在趋势检测中分析局部秩统计量。

Result: 1) 获得了窗口函数空间的正交分解，每层对应不同的重叠水平；2) 给出了提取各成分分量的显式过程；3) 揭示了不同统计量具有不同的局部分辨尺度，包括非经典尺度；4) 提供了衡量增量信息的定量指标，可用于指导窗口尺度的选择。

Conclusion: 本文为重叠滑动窗口检验提供了系统的设计与分析方法，包括渐近正态化和局部行为推导，为依赖性和趋势检测提供了统一的数学框架。

Abstract: We develop a general framework for constructing and analyzing overlapping sliding-window statistics for dependence and trend detection. For a fixed window size, the overlapping blocks form a Markov chain, and the asymptotic variance of any centered window statistic is determined by the covariance operator of this chain. Using its spectral structure, we obtain an orthogonal decomposition of the space of window functions into components associated with different overlap levels. This leads to a natural notion of incremental dependence information: the part of a statistic that captures exactly the new information introduced by enlarging the window.
  We give an explicit procedure for extracting these components and apply the method to two classes of examples. For correlation detection, we study symmetric polynomial window functions and identify their informative projected part. For trend detection, we analyze localized rank-based statistics and isolate the contribution of the largest newly introduced lag. The examples also show that different statistics may exhibit different local detection scales, including nonclassical ones. The same viewpoint leads to a natural quantitative measure of incremental information, which can be used to assess how much new dependence structure is captured as the window size increases, and to guide scale selection. Overall, the paper provides a systematic method for designing overlapping-window tests and deriving their asymptotic normalization and local behavior.

</details>


### [168] [A unified approach for testing in Hilbert spaces on incomplete data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13209)
*Daniel Gaigall,Philipp Wübbolding*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在可分希尔伯特空间中基于不完全观测进行统计检验的通用框架，适用于高维和函数型数据，涵盖拟合优度、对称性、同质性和独立性检验，并通过正态性检验实例展示了新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代应用中常出现高维随机向量缺失条目或部分观测随机过程等不完全数据，但现有统计检验方法缺乏一个统一的希尔伯特空间框架来处理这类问题。

Method: 在可分希尔伯特空间中建模不完全数据，识别其独立同分布结构，借助大数定律和中心极限定理等数学工具进行分析，并提出了一种通用的假设检验概念。

Result: 所提框架能统一处理多种数据类型（高维数据和函数型数据）及多种检验问题（拟合优度、对称性、同质性、独立性），并以正态性检验为例展示了方法的具体应用。

Conclusion: 该研究为不完全数据下的统计检验提供了理论严谨的通用解决方案，有望在超高维随机向量和部分观测随机过程等实际场景中广泛应用。

Abstract: We consider statistical testing on the basis of incomplete observations with values in a separable Hilbert space, where the dimension is possibly large or even infinite. The general Hilbert space setting allows various data types as they arise in modern applications, in particular high dimensional and functional data. Possible Hilbert space testing problems are goodness-of-fit, symmetry, homogeneity and independence. We present an approach for modeling incomplete data that covers several problems in practice, e.g., ultra high dimensional random vectors with missing entries or partially observed stochastic processes. We identify a specific structure (independent and identically distributed) in the incomplete data that enables the analysis of statistical procedures with the help of suitable mathematical results (e.g., laws of large numbers and central limit theorems). Additionally, a general and novel concept for testing different hypotheses in this situation is suggested and sketched for the example of testing goodness-of-fit for normality.

</details>


### [169] [On the existence and non-existence of centres of mass on Hilbert spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13667)
*Shahin Tavakoli*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 论文证明了无限维球面上Fr​​échet均值和L^p质心不一定存在，并给出了存在性的简单条件；此外证明了样本质心的优化问题可降维至最多n维子球面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无限维球面上，质心（Fr​​échet均值和L^p中心）的存在性尚未被证明，而有限维情形下由紧性保证存在。本研究旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过反例证明无限维球面上质心不一定存在；给出一个简单条件确保存在性；利用几何论证将样本质心优化问题约化到由数据点张成的有限维子球面上。

Result: 无限维球面上质心未必存在；在特定条件下存在性可保证；样本质心的求解可降维至不超过n维的子球面。

Conclusion: 揭示了无限维球面与有限维球面在质心存在性上的本质差异，并提供了实用的降维计算方法，具有统计推断方面的应用意义。

Abstract: Fréchet means and $L^p$ centres of mass provide notions of average location in metric spaces. On finite-dimensional spheres, existence follows from compactness.
  On infinite-dimensional spheres, it is not known whether a centre of mass always exists. We show that this is not always the case, and give a simple assumption under which a centre of mass exists. We then show that finding the sample centre of mass of data $x_1, \ldots, x_n$ on the sphere is always an optimisation problem on a subsphere of manifold dimension at most $n$, regardless of the potentially infinite dimension of the sphere. We conclude with some statistical implications.

</details>


### [170] [Nonstandard likelihood-ratio limits under semidefinite rank constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13761)
*Didier Concordet*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了半正定矩阵秩假设的似然比检验，发现零假设分层导致不同秩层面的不同极限分布，并证明了在特定条件下的最不利分布性质及形状导数结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有似然比检验在零假设为分层结构（矩阵秩不超过某值）时，仅考虑顶层正则边界的卡方-棒-平方校准不足以描述整个复合零假设，尤其是当秩在$n^{-1/2}$尺度局部变化时，需要更完整的理论框架。

Method: 对正则 nuisance 参数进行剖面后，为每个固定零秩和所有允许的局部秩过渡导出简化高斯实验。考虑各向同性假设下的最不利分布性质，以及各向异性下主动余秩为1时的过渡优势。在顶层边界推导了条件形状导数。

Result: 在顶层边界恢复经典卡方-棒-平方分布；低秩时极限分布涉及非凸秩约束半正定集的投影。各向同性下顶层分布是所有固定零秩和局部秩过渡中最不利的；各向异性下主动余秩为1时成立过渡优势。获得了顶层边界极限分布及其临界值的条件形状导数。

Conclusion: 为半正定矩阵秩假设的似然比检验提供了完整的渐近理论，揭示了分层零假设下不同秩层面的极限行为差异，给出了保守检验的校准依据。

Abstract: We study likelihood-ratio tests for the hypothesis that a positive-semidefinite matrix has rank at most a prescribed value. The null hypothesis is stratified: points of maximal allowed rank lie on a regular boundary stratum, whereas lower-rank points are singular. Consequently, the usual chi-bar-square calibration on the top stratum does not by itself describe the whole composite null, especially along sequences whose rank changes at the local $n^{-1/2}$ scale.
  After profiling regular nuisance parameters, we derive a common reduced Gaussian experiment for every fixed null rank and for all admissible local rank transitions. On the top stratum, the classical chi-bar-square law is recovered. At lower ranks, the limit generally involves projection onto a nonconvex rank-constrained semidefinite set.
  Our main calibration result shows that, under isotropy, the top-stratum law is least favourable over all fixed null strata and all local null rank transitions. We also prove the corresponding transition dominance under arbitrary anisotropy when the active corank is one. Finally, on the top stratum, we obtain a conditional shape derivative for the limiting distribution and its critical value. Gaussian covariance models and finite-sample experiments illustrate nuisance profiling, rank transitions, anisotropy, and orientation sensitivity.

</details>


### [171] [A Frequentist Approach to Change Point Detection: Methods and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13852)
*Debanjana Datta*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了函数型时间序列中的变点检测问题，允许观测在稀疏和密集支撑上变化，同时处理均值偏移和过程波动性。方法基于给定所有其他参数条件下变点的条件概率最大化，并证明了所提估计量的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数型时间序列中，观测数据可能在稀疏和密集支撑上变化，现有的变点检测方法难以同时处理这两种情况以及均值偏移和过程波动性问题。

Method: 基于条件概率最大化的方法，即在给定所有其他参数条件下，最大化变点的条件概率来估计变点位置。

Result: 证明了所提出的估计量具有一致性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于条件概率最大化的变点检测方法能有效处理函数型时间序列在稀疏和密集支撑下的均值偏移和过程波动性变点检测问题，且估计量具有一致性。

Abstract: In this paper we study the problem of change point detection in functional time series where the observations are allowed to vary on both sparse and dense support. We address the problem of mean shift as well as the process volatility. Our methodology is based on the maximization of the conditional probability of change point given all the other parameters. Further, it has been proved that the proposed estimator is consistent.

</details>


### [172] [Partially Correlated Verifier Cascades in LLM Harnesses: Concave Log-Odds, Polynomial Reliability, and Blind-Spot Ceilings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13918)
*Jiangang Han*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种部分相关验证器级联的理论框架，将每个实例的误接受率建模为潜变量α∼G，证明了后验对数几率ℓ_k = ℓ₀ - ln m_k（m_k为G的k阶矩），揭示了：ℓ_k对k呈凹性（Odds Law为其上界）、Beta分布下失败呈多项式衰减、盲点原子导致可靠性饱和、以及真实接受率变化时出现三种渐近行为。通过重复判决可估计G的前R阶矩，实验表明独立性假设会严重低估失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对序列验证门（serial verification gates）在条件独立假设下已由Odds Law给出指数级失效衰减，但部分相关验证器级联的理论仍然缺失的问题，本文旨在建立最小化的部分相关理论框架。

Method: 将每个实例的误接受率建模为服从分布G的潜变量α（de Finetti风格），推导出精确级联后验ℓ_k = ℓ₀ - ln m_k；使用Beta分布、盲点原子等具体模型进行分析；引入真实接受率β∼H的变体；通过重复判决进行矩估计。

Result: (i) ℓ_k对k凹性，Odds Law为其上界；(ii) Beta(a,b)下失败多项式衰减1-r_k ∝ k^{-b}，相关性参数ρ_v=1/(a+b+1)；(iii) 盲点原子α=1处质量1-π使证据上限为-ln(1-π) nats；(iv) 真实接受率变化时出现三种渐近行为。合成测试中独立性假设低估失败率达20倍(k=5)和3000倍(k=10)。

Conclusion: 验证器相关性会显著降低级联可靠性，独立性假设会严重乐观地估计性能；实际有效手段是去相关（改变模型族、模态或证据源），而非简单增加验证门数量。

Abstract: Serial verification gates are a core reliability primitive in LLM harnesses: a candidate answer is returned only if $k$ verifier calls all accept it. Under conditionally independent gates, the recent Odds Law (arXiv:2606.15712) shows that posterior log-odds grow linearly in $k$, so failure decays exponentially, and states that "a tight theory of partially correlated verifier cascades remains open." This note gives a minimal such theory. Modeling the per-instance false-accept rate on the generator's own errors as a latent variable $α\sim G$ (de Finetti), the exact cascade posterior is $\ell_k = \ell_0 - \ln m_k$, with $m_k$ the $k$-th moment of $G$. Then: (i) $\ell_k$ is concave in $k$ for every non-degenerate $G$ -- the Odds Law is its tangent at the first gate and an upper bound; (ii) for Beta$(a,b)$ latents, failure decays polynomially, $1-r_k \asymp k^{-b}$, with correlation parameter $ρ_v = 1/(a+b+1)$; (iii) a blind-spot atom of mass $1-π$ at $α=1$ caps the evidence extractable from any number of gates at $-\ln(1-π)$ nats, so reliability saturates below 1; (iv) letting the true-accept rate also vary ($β\sim H$) yields a trichotomy -- gates eventually always help, plateau, or actively harm -- decided by the upper-tail exponents of $G$ and $H$, with closed-form crossover $k^\dagger$. The mechanism is survivorship: errors surviving gates are the high-$α$ ones. The theory is measurable: $R$ repeated verdicts per instance identify the first $R$ moments of $G$, so two verdicts identify $ρ_v$; beta-binomial likelihood and NPMLE recover the reliability curve and the ill-posed ceiling. In synthetic tests, independence-based extrapolation underestimates failure by 20x at $k=5$ and ~3000x at $k=10$; the correlated fit at $R=8$ tracks held-out depths. The practical lever is decorrelation -- changing model family, modality, or evidence source -- not adding gates.

</details>


### [173] [Minimax Theory of Likelihood-Based Deep Learning for Speckle Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14064)
*Soham Jana*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了乘性散斑噪声下基于深度神经网络的非参数回归估计的极小极大最优性，证明了在同时存在乘性散斑噪声和加性高斯噪声时，DNN估计器的收敛速率与仅存在加性高斯噪声时一致（仅相差对数因子），表明乘性噪声并未本质增加估计难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习方法在散斑去噪实践中取得了最优性能，但其基本统计极限尚未被充分探索。与加性噪声不同，乘性散斑噪声使得回归函数无法通过条件均值识别，导致传统基于最小二乘的深度学习方法不可行。

Method: 研究了同时包含乘性散斑噪声和加性高斯噪声的模型下，基于似然的深度神经网络（DNN）估计器的极小极大估计。该框架同时适用于低维和稀疏高维特征。推导了DNN估计器在有限样本下的误差上界，并建立了非参数函数恢复的极小极大下界。

Result: 提出的上界与下界在样本量的对数因子内相匹配。这些极小极大速率与仅存在加性高斯噪声下的非参数回归速率在对数因子内一致，表明乘性散斑噪声并未改变估计的内在困难程度。数值实验支持DNN去噪方法的一致性并证明了其有效性。

Conclusion: 乘性散斑噪声虽然使得回归函数不可识别于条件均值，但基于似然的DNN估计器在统计收敛速率上可以达到与仅存在加性高斯噪声时相同的水平，表明散斑去噪问题的统计难度并未因噪声的乘性性质而增加。

Abstract: Speckle noise is a multiplicative noise commonly encountered in coherent imaging modalities such as synthetic aperture radar, optical coherence tomography, and digital holography. Although deep learning methods, in practice, have achieved state-of-the-art performance for speckle denoising, their fundamental statistical limits remain largely unexplored. Unlike additive noise models, multiplicative speckle noise makes the regression function unidentifiable from the conditional mean, rendering conventional least-squares-based deep learning approaches inapplicable.
  We study the minimax estimation of smooth nonparametric regression functions using likelihood-based deep neural network (DNN) estimators under a model with both multiplicative speckle noise and additive Gaussian noise. Our framework accommodates both low-dimensional and sparse high-dimensional features. We establish finite-sample upper bounds on the estimation error of the proposed DNN estimators and derive minimax lower bounds for nonparametric function recovery under our model, showing that they match up to logarithmic factors in the sample size. Moreover, these minimax rates coincide, up to logarithmic factors, with those for nonparametric regression under additive Gaussian noise alone, demonstrating that the intrinsic difficulty of estimation remains essentially unchanged despite the challenges posed by multiplicative speckle noise. Numerical experiments further supports consistency of our DNN-based despeckling methods and demonstrate their effectiveness.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Prospective clinical indication, post-hoc report leakage, and fusion design in multi-image chest radiograph classification: a patient-clustered evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13800)
*Kamran Shahid,Muhammad Munwar Iqbal*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了15,000个ReXGradient-160K胸部X光片研究，比较了不同模态融合策略。结果表明：临床指征与报告衍生标签高度相关，置换感知融合方法具有竞争力，而事后报告文本存在严重的报告-标签循环性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部X光片数据集常将不同护理阶段产生的多种输入（临床指征、发现、印象）与图像混合使用，但这种方式可能引入标签循环性偏差。研究旨在系统评估不同模态输入的前瞻性预测价值，区分真正的预测信号与事后文本中的循环性信息。

Method: 使用15,000个ReXGradient-160K研究，每个研究包含两张可读图像和五个CheXbert衍生报告观察。采用冻结的DenseNet-121和Bio+ClinicalBERT编码器，比较了纯图像、纯指征、固定顺序多模态、随机交换、DeepSets和SectionGuard-MI等模型。使用五个种子训练，通过2,000个患者聚类bootstrap重复估计公开测试不确定性。

Result: 在U-Ones下，宏AUROC：单张图像0.643，两张图像0.694，临床指征0.749，普通两图像+指征融合0.780。SectionGuard-MI达到AUROC 0.783和AUPRC 0.260。DeepSets前瞻性AUROC最高(0.787)，随机交换融合前瞻性AUPRC最高(0.265)且校准更好。完整报告文本AUROC达0.979，AUPRC达0.836，即使精确或扩展掩码后AUROC仍高于0.973。

Conclusion: 前瞻性临床指征与报告衍生目标高度相关；置换感知融合方法具有竞争力；事后报告文本存在严重的报告-标签循环性，会高估模型性能。

Abstract: Chest radiograph datasets often combine multiple images with Clinical Indication, Findings, and Impression, although these inputs are produced at different stages of care. We evaluated 15,000 ReXGradient-160K studies with two readable images and five CheXbert-derived report observations. Frozen DenseNet-121 and Bio+ClinicalBERT encoders were used to compare image-only, Indication-only, fixed-order multimodal, random-swap, DeepSets, and SectionGuard-MI models. Findings and Impression were evaluated only as post-hoc leakage controls. Models were trained with five seeds, and public-test uncertainty was estimated with 2,000 patient-cluster bootstrap replicates. Under U-Ones, macro AUROC was 0.643 for the primary image, 0.694 for two images, 0.749 for Indication, and 0.780 for ordinary two-image-plus-Indication fusion. SectionGuard-MI achieved AUROC 0.783 and AUPRC 0.260. Relative to ordinary fusion, its paired AUROC difference was 0.0031 (95% CI, -0.0042 to 0.0104; adjusted p=0.374), while its AUPRC difference was 0.0289 (95% CI, 0.0095 to 0.0413; adjusted p=0.004). DeepSets had the highest prospective AUROC point estimate (0.787), and random-swap fusion had the highest prospective AUPRC point estimate (0.265) with better calibration than SectionGuard-MI. Full report text alone reached AUROC 0.979 and AUPRC 0.836; AUROC remained above 0.973 after exact or expanded masking. These results show that prospective Indication is strongly associated with report-derived targets, permutation-aware fusion is competitive, and post-hoc report text creates substantial report-label circularity.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)
